#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct IpAttempt {
    failed_count: u32,
    window_start: Instant,
    blocked_until: Option<Instant>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct LoginRateLimiter {
    records: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, IpAttempt>>>,
}

impl LoginRateLimiter {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            records: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Check if IP has exceeded 5 failed attempts in 10 minutes and is blocked
    pub async fn check_ip(&self, ip: &str) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let mut map = self.records.lock().await;
        let now = Instant::now();

        if let Some(entry) = map.get_mut(ip) {
            if let Some(blocked_until) = entry.blocked_until {
                if now < blocked_until {
                    let rem_secs = blocked_until.duration_since(now).as_secs();
                    return Err(CoreError::TooManyRequests(format!(
                        "Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ûµ ØªÙ„Ø§Ø´ Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ Ù…Ú©Ø±Ø±ØŒ Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ Ø§Ø² Ø§ÛŒÙ† IP Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ {} Ø«Ø§Ù†ÛŒÙ‡ Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø± ØªÙ„Ø§Ø´ ÙØ±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.",
                        rem_secs
                    )));
                } else {
                    entry.blocked_until = None;
                    entry.failed_count = 0;
                    entry.window_start = now;
                }
            }

            if now.duration_since(entry.window_start).as_secs() > 600 {
                entry.failed_count = 0;
                entry.window_start = now;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn record_failure(&self, ip: &str) {
        let mut map = self.records.lock().await;
        let now = Instant::now();
        let entry = map.entry(ip.to_string()).or_insert(IpAttempt {
            failed_count: 0,
            window_start: now,
            blocked_until: None,
        });

        if now.duration_since(entry.window_start).as_secs() > 600 {
            entry.failed_count = 1;
            entry.window_start = now;
        } else {
            entry.failed_count += 1;
        }

        if entry.failed_count >= 5 {
            entry.blocked_until = Some(now + std::time::Duration::from_secs(900));
            tracing::warn!("IP {} exceeded 5 failed login attempts in 10 minutes. Blocked for 15 minutes.", ip);
        }
    }

    pub async fn record_success(&self, ip: &str) {
        let mut map = self.records.lock().await;
        map.remove(ip);
    }
}

fn extract_client_ip(headers: &HeaderMap) -> String {
    if let Some(xff) = headers.get("x-forwarded-for") {
        if let Ok(s) = xff.to_str() {
            if let Some(first) = s.split(',').next() {
                let trimmed = first.trim();
                if !trimmed.is_empty() {
                    return trimmed.to_string();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(xreal) = headers.get("x-real-ip") {
        if let Ok(s) = xreal.to_str() {
            let trimmed = s.trim();
            if !trimmed.is_empty() {
                return trimmed.to_string();
            }
        }
    }
    "127.0.0.1".to_string()
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AppState {
    pub pool: DbPool,
    pub anomaly_detector: crate::anomaly_detector::AnomalyDetector,
    pub bot: Option<teloxide::Bot>,
    pub admin_chat_id: i64,
    pub rate_limiter: LoginRateLimiter,
    pub server_host: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct CodeLoginRequest {
    pub code: String,
    pub device_identifier: Option<String>,
    pub device_name: Option<String>,
    pub os: Option<String>,
    pub client_version: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct RegisterRequest {
    pub email: Option<String>,
    pub username: Option<String>,
    pub password: String,
    pub device_identifier: Option<String>,
    pub device_name: Option<String>,
    pub os: Option<String>,
    pub client_version: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct LoginRequest {
    pub email: Option<String>,
    pub username: Option<String>,
    pub password: String,
    pub device_identifier: Option<String>,
    pub device_name: Option<String>,
    pub os: Option<String>,
    pub client_version: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct HeartbeatRequest {
    pub token: Option<String>,
    pub device_id: String,
    pub mode: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub delta_upload_bytes: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub delta_download_bytes: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub struct ConfigRequest {
    pub token: Option<String>,
    pub device_id: Option<String>,
    pub os: Option<String>,
    pub mode: Option<String>,
    pub protocol: Option<String>,
    pub location_code: Option<String>,
    pub use_warp: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct SubQuery {
    pub format: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct ModesQuery {
    pub token: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct OvpnQuery {
    pub mode: Option<String>,
    pub protocol: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct RemoveDeviceRequest {
    pub device_id: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct TelemetryReportRequest {
    pub token: String,
    pub bytes_delta: i64,
    pub device_id: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct OpenVpnAuthRequest {
    pub username: String,
    pub password: Option<String>,
    pub mode: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct OpenVpnAccountingRequest {
    pub token: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bytes_sent: u64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bytes_received: u64,
    pub mode: Option<String>,
}

pub fn create_router(
    pool: DbPool,
    anomaly_detector: crate::anomaly_detector::AnomalyDetector,
    bot: Option<teloxide::Bot>,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    server_host: String,
) -> Router {
    let state = AppState {
        pool,
        anomaly_detector,
        bot,
        admin_chat_id,
        rate_limiter: LoginRateLimiter::new(),
        server_host,
    };

    let cors = CorsLayer::new()
        .allow_origin(Any)
        .allow_methods(Any)
        .allow_headers(Any);

    Router::new()
        // 1. Auth endpoints
        .route("/api/v1/auth/register", post(auth_register))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/login", post(auth_login))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/request-telegram-verify", post(auth_request_telegram_verify))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/verify-status", get(auth_verify_status))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/code-login", post(auth_code_login))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/exchange-code", post(auth_code_login))
        .route("/api/v1/auth/link-token", post(auth_link_token))
        // 2. Client Connection Modes & Locations catalog
        .route("/api/v1/client/modes", get(get_client_modes))
        .route("/api/v1/client/locations", get(get_client_locations))
        // 3. Client Sing-box dynamic config compiler
        .route("/api/v1/client/config", post(client_config))
        .route("/api/v1/client/connect", post(client_config))
        // 4. Device Management (Max 3 Devices)
        .route("/api/v1/client/devices", get(get_client_devices))
        .route("/api/v1/client/devices/remove", post(remove_client_device))
        .route("/api/v1/client/devices/:device_id", delete(delete_client_device_by_path))
        // 5. Periodic Heartbeat & Instant Session Revocation
        .route("/api/v1/client/heartbeat", post(client_heartbeat))
        // 6. Client Version Check & Update Notification
        .route("/api/v1/client/version", get(client_version))
        // 7. Live Telemetry & Traffic Anomaly Detection
        .route("/api/v1/client/telemetry", post(client_telemetry))
        // 8. V2Ray / Sing-box Subscription endpoints
        .route("/api/v1/sub/:token", get(get_subscription))
        .route("/sub/:token", get(get_subscription))
        // 9. OpenVPN profile generator & internal daemon hooks
        .route("/api/v1/ovpn/:token", get(get_openvpn_profile))
        .route("/api/v1/mobileconfig/:token", get(get_mobileconfig_profile))
        .route("/api/internal/openvpn/auth", post(openvpn_internal_auth))
        .route("/api/internal/openvpn/accounting", post(openvpn_internal_accounting))
        .route("/api/internal/ikev2/auth", post(ikev2_internal_auth))
        .route("/api/internal/ikev2/accounting", post(ikev2_internal_accounting))
        // 10. System Health check
        .route("/health", get(health_check))
        .layer(cors)
        .layer(TraceLayer::new_for_http())
        .with_state(state)
}

/// Helper function to extract auth / subscription token from headers
fn extract_auth_token(headers: &HeaderMap) -> Result<String, CoreError> {
    headers
        .get("X-Subscription-Token")
        .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
        .map(|s| s.to_string())
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.strip_prefix("jwt_sentra_").unwrap_or(s).to_string())
        })
        .ok_or(CoreError::AuthFailed("ØªÙˆÚ©Ù† ÛŒØ§ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()))
}

/// POST /api/v1/auth/register
/// Registers a new account directly with email & password (or username), provisions Lite subscription, and registers device
async fn auth_register(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Json(payload): Json<RegisterRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let client_ip = extract_client_ip(&headers);
    state.rate_limiter.check_ip(&client_ip).await?;

    let password = payload.password.trim();
    if password.len() < 8 {
        state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
        return Err(CoreError::BadRequest(
            "Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ù‚Ù„ Û¸ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø§Ú©ØªØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.".to_string(),
        ));
    }

    // Rate limit: Max 2 registrations per 24 hours per IP
    Repository::check_and_record_ip_registration(&state.pool, &client_ip).await?;

    let (user, sub) = if let Some(ref em) = payload.email {
        let clean_email = em.trim().to_lowercase();
        match Repository::register_user_with_email_and_password(&state.pool, &clean_email, password, None).await {
            Ok(res) => {
                state.rate_limiter.record_success(&client_ip).await;
                res
            }
            Err(e) => {
                state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
                return Err(e);
            }
        }
    } else if let Some(ref un) = payload.username {
        let clean_un = un.trim().to_lowercase();
        if clean_un.contains('@') {
            match Repository::register_user_with_email_and_password(&state.pool, &clean_un, password, None).await {
                Ok(res) => {
                    state.rate_limiter.record_success(&client_ip).await;
                    res
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
                    return Err(e);
                }
            }
        } else {
            match Repository::register_user_with_credentials(&state.pool, &clean_un, password, None).await {
                Ok(res) => {
                    state.rate_limiter.record_success(&client_ip).await;
                    res
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
                    return Err(e);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
        return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø§ÛŒÙ…ÛŒÙ„ Ùˆ Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ ÙØ±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.".to_string()));
    };

    let device_id = payload.device_identifier.as_deref().unwrap_or("desktop-client-001");
    let platform = payload.os.as_deref().unwrap_or("windows");
    let device_name = payload.device_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(match platform {
        "android" => "Android Mobile",
        "ios" => "iPhone / iPad",
        "darwin" | "macos" => "Apple Mac",
        "linux" => "Linux PC",
        _ => "Windows PC",
    });

    Repository::register_or_update_device(
        &state.pool,
        user.id,
        sub.id,
        device_id,
        device_name,
        platform,
    )
    .await?;

    let remaining_bytes = (sub.traffic_limit_bytes - (sub.upload_bytes + sub.download_bytes)).max(0);

    Ok((
        StatusCode::CREATED,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "access_token": format!("jwt_sentra_{}", sub.token),
            "token_type": "Bearer",
            "telegram_verified": false,
            "user": {
                "id": user.id,
                "user_uuid": user.user_uuid,
                "email": user.email,
                "telegram_id": user.telegram_id,
                "telegram_verified": false,
                "username": user.username,
                "first_name": user.first_name,
                "role": user.role
            },
            "subscription": {
                "tier": sub.tier,
                "token": sub.token,
                "status": sub.status,
                "remaining_bytes": remaining_bytes,
                "max_concurrent_devices": sub.max_concurrent_devices,
                "expires_at": sub.expires_at.to_rfc3339()
            }
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/auth/login
/// Validates email/username & password credentials, verifies device slot (Max 3 Devices), and returns session JWT + telegram_verified status
async fn auth_login(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Json(payload): Json<LoginRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let client_ip = extract_client_ip(&headers);
    state.rate_limiter.check_ip(&client_ip).await?;

    let identifier = payload.email.as_deref()
        .or(payload.username.as_deref())
        .map(|s| s.trim())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .ok_or_else(|| CoreError::BadRequest("Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø§ÛŒÙ…ÛŒÙ„ Ùˆ Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ ÙØ±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.".to_string()))?;

    let password = payload.password.trim();

    let (user, sub) = match Repository::login_user_with_email_and_password(&state.pool, identifier, password).await {
        Ok(res) => {
            state.rate_limiter.record_success(&client_ip).await;
            res
        }
        Err(e) => {
            state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
            return Err(e);
        }
    };

    let device_id = payload.device_identifier.as_deref().unwrap_or("desktop-client-001");
    let platform = payload.os.as_deref().unwrap_or("windows");
    let device_name = payload.device_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(match platform {
        "android" => "Android Mobile",
        "ios" => "iPhone / iPad",
        "darwin" | "macos" => "Apple Mac",
        "linux" => "Linux PC",
        _ => "Windows PC",
    });

    Repository::register_or_update_device(
        &state.pool,
        user.id,
        sub.id,
        device_id,
        device_name,
        platform,
    )
    .await?;

    let remaining_bytes = (sub.traffic_limit_bytes - (sub.upload_bytes + sub.download_bytes)).max(0);
    let is_verified = user.telegram_verified != 0;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "access_token": format!("jwt_sentra_{}", sub.token),
            "token_type": "Bearer",
            "telegram_verified": is_verified,
            "user": {
                "id": user.id,
                "user_uuid": user.user_uuid,
                "email": user.email,
                "telegram_id": user.telegram_id,
                "telegram_verified": is_verified,
                "username": user.username,
                "first_name": user.first_name,
                "role": user.role
            },
            "subscription": {
                "tier": sub.tier,
                "token": sub.token,
                "status": sub.status,
                "remaining_bytes": remaining_bytes,
                "max_concurrent_devices": sub.max_concurrent_devices,
                "expires_at": sub.expires_at.to_rfc3339()
            }
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/auth/request-telegram-verify
/// Generates temporary 6-digit verification code with 15-minute expiration and direct Telegram bot link
async fn auth_request_telegram_verify(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = extract_auth_token(&headers)?;
    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;
    let user = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or(CoreError::UserNotFound)?;

    let code = Repository::request_telegram_verify(&state.pool, user.id).await?;
    let bot_username = std::env::var("TELOXIDE_BOT_USERNAME").unwrap_or_else(|_| "SentraVpnBot".to_string());
    let verify_url = format!("https://t.me/{}?start=verify_{}", bot_username, code);

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "verify_code": code,
            "bot_username": bot_username,
            "verify_url": verify_url,
            "expires_in_seconds": 900
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// GET /api/v1/auth/verify-status
/// Returns current Telegram verification state for the authenticated user
async fn auth_verify_status(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = extract_auth_token(&headers)?;
    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;
    let user = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or(CoreError::UserNotFound)?;

    let is_verified = user.telegram_verified != 0;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "telegram_verified": is_verified,
            "telegram_id": user.telegram_id,
            "email": user.email,
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/auth/link-token
/// Generates temporary 6-digit link code for linking desktop account to Telegram bot
async fn auth_link_token(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = headers
        .get("X-Subscription-Token")
        .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
        .map(|s| s.to_string())
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.strip_prefix("jwt_sentra_").unwrap_or(s).to_string())
        })
        .ok_or(CoreError::AuthFailed("ØªÙˆÚ©Ù† ÛŒØ§ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø³Øª Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()))?;

    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;
    let link_code = Repository::generate_link_code(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "link_code": link_code,
            "expires_in_seconds": 300,
            "instructions": "Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ú©Ø¯ Û¶ Ø±Ù‚Ù…ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ø¸Ø±Ù Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡ Ø¯Ø± Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… Ø¨Ø§ Ø¯Ø³ØªÙˆØ± /link ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ ÙØ±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯."
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/auth/code-login
/// Validates 8-digit OTP code, registers user device (Max 3 Devices), and returns session JWT
async fn auth_code_login(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Json(payload): Json<CodeLoginRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let client_ip = extract_client_ip(&headers);
    state.rate_limiter.check_ip(&client_ip).await?;

    let code = payload.code.trim();
    if code.len() != 8 {
        state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
        return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ú©Ø¯ ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ø§Ù‹ Û¸ Ø±Ù‚Ù… Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.".to_string()));
    }

    let device_id = payload.device_identifier.as_deref().unwrap_or("desktop-client-001");
    let device_name = payload.device_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Sentra Connect Device");
    let os = payload.os.as_deref().unwrap_or("windows");
    let version = payload.client_version.as_deref().unwrap_or("1.0.0");

    let (user, sub) = match Repository::validate_and_exchange_code(
        &state.pool,
        code,
        device_id,
        device_name,
        os,
        version,
        &client_ip,
    )
    .await
    {
        Ok(res) => {
            state.rate_limiter.record_success(&client_ip).await;
            res
        }
        Err(e) => {
            state.rate_limiter.record_failure(&client_ip).await;
            return Err(e);
        }
    };

    let remaining_bytes = (sub.traffic_limit_bytes - (sub.upload_bytes + sub.download_bytes)).max(0);

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "access_token": format!("jwt_sentra_{}", sub.token),
            "token_type": "Bearer",
            "user": {
                "id": user.id,
                "telegram_id": user.telegram_id,
                "username": user.username,
                "first_name": user.first_name,
                "role": user.role
            },
            "subscription": {
                "tier": sub.tier,
                "token": sub.token,
                "status": sub.status,
                "remaining_bytes": remaining_bytes,
                "max_concurrent_devices": sub.max_concurrent_devices,
                "expires_at": sub.expires_at.to_rfc3339()
            }
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// GET /api/v1/client/modes
/// Returns the 4 connection modes with user tier lock state, quota and descriptions
async fn get_client_modes(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Query(query): Query<ModesQuery>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = query.token
        .or_else(|| {
            headers.get("X-Subscription-Token")
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .map(|s| s.to_string())
        })
        .or_else(|| {
            headers.get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.replace("jwt_sentra_", ""))
        });

    let (modes, is_verified) = if let Some(t) = token {
        match Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &t).await {
            Ok(mut sub) => {
                let _ = Repository::check_and_reset_daily_quota(&state.pool, &mut sub).await;
                let verified = if let Ok(Some(u)) = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await {
                    u.telegram_verified != 0
                } else {
                    false
                };
                (Repository::build_modes_info(&sub, verified), verified)
            }
            Err(_) => (Repository::build_default_modes_info(), false),
        }
    } else {
        (Repository::build_default_modes_info(), false)
    };

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "telegram_verified": is_verified,
            "modes": modes
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// GET /api/v1/client/locations
/// Returns active online locations with ping and node count for client Location Switcher
async fn get_client_locations(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let locations = Repository::get_active_locations_for_free_modes(&state.pool).await?;
    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "default": "auto",
            "locations": locations
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/client/config
/// Generates valid Sing-box v1.10+ JSON according to PROTOCOL_SPEC.md tailored to user's tier & mode
async fn client_config(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Json(payload): Json<ConfigRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    // Extract token from request JSON body or HTTP headers
    let token = if let Some(ref t) = payload.token {
        t.clone()
    } else if let Some(h) = headers.get("X-Subscription-Token") {
        h.to_str().map_err(|_| CoreError::AuthFailed("Invalid header value".to_string()))?.to_string()
    } else if let Some(auth_h) = headers.get(header::AUTHORIZATION) {
        let auth_str = auth_h.to_str().unwrap_or("");
        if let Some(token_str) = auth_str.strip_prefix("Bearer ") {
            token_str.replace("jwt_sentra_", "")
        } else {
            return Err(CoreError::SubscriptionNotFound);
        }
    } else {
        return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Subscription token must be provided in request body or headers.".to_string()));
    };

    let user_opt = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;

    }

        return Err(CoreError::QuotaExceeded {
            used: used_bytes,
            limit: sub.traffic_limit_bytes,
        });
    }

    let platform = payload.os.as_deref().unwrap_or("windows");
    let device_name = match platform {
        "android" => "Android Mobile",
        "ios" => "iPhone / iPad",
        "darwin" | "macos" => "Apple Mac",
        "linux" => "Linux PC",
        _ => "Windows PC",
    };

            mode: "Ú¯ÛŒÙ…ÛŒÙ†Ú¯ (Gaming)".to_string(),
        });

                return Err(CoreError::ModeLocked {
                    mode: "Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯ (Download) - Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø² Ø¨Ù‡ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø¯Ø± Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù…".to_string(),

                mode: "Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª (Static IP)".to_string(),
            });
        }
        if sub.static_ip_used_mb >= 30_720 {
            return Err(CoreError::StaticIpQuotaExceeded {
                used_mb: sub.static_ip_used_mb,
                limit_mb: 30_720,
            });
        }
    }

        "openvpn_tcp" | "tcp" => "openvpn_tcp",
        "stealth" => "stealth",
        "ikev2" => "ikev2",
        _ => "openvpn_udp",
    };

        "127.0.0.1".to_string()
    } else {

    };

        "stealth" => 11940,
        "openvpn_tcp" => match requested_mode {

            _ => 443,
        },
        _ => match requested_mode {
            "download" => 1195,
            "static_ip" => 1196,
            _ => 1194,
        },
    };

    let ovpn_config = compiler::generate_ovpn_profile_ext(&sub, Some(requested_mode), requested_protocol, &server_host);

        Some(json!({
            "host": server_host,
            "port": 443,
            "sni": format!("{}.sentravpn.com", requested_mode)

    };

            "server_host": server_host,
            "remote_id": server_host,
            "eap_username": sub.token,
            "eap_password": sub.token,
            "ca_cert": "-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----\nMIIDSzCCAjOgAwIBAgIUW8d0Q3k9M1A2B3C4D5E6F7A8B9C0...[SENTRA-ROOT-CA]...\n-----END CERTIFICATE-----",

    };

        "FI" => "ÙÙ†Ù„Ø§Ù†Ø¯ ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡®",
        "FR" => "ÙØ±Ø§Ù†Ø³Ù‡ ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡·",
        "GB" | "UK" => "Ø§Ù†Ú¯Ù„Ø³ØªØ§Ù† ðŸ‡¬ðŸ‡§",

    };

        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],

            "protocol": requested_protocol,
            "mode": requested_mode,

/// GET /api/v1/client/devices
/// Returns list of registered devices for the authenticated user (Max 3 Devices)
async fn get_client_devices(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Query(query): Query<ModesQuery>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = query.token
        .or_else(|| {
            headers.get("X-Subscription-Token")
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .map(|s| s.to_string())
        })
        .or_else(|| {
            headers.get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.replace("jwt_sentra_", ""))
        })
        .ok_or(CoreError::SubscriptionNotFound)?;

    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;
    let devices = Repository::get_user_devices(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "devices": devices,
            "count": devices.len(),
            "max_allowed": 3
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/client/devices/remove
/// Removes a registered device to free up slot for a new device
async fn remove_client_device(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Query(query): Query<ModesQuery>,
    Json(payload): Json<RemoveDeviceRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = query
        .token
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get("X-Subscription-Token")
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .map(|s| s.to_string())
        })
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.replace("jwt_sentra_", ""))
        })
        .ok_or(CoreError::SubscriptionNotFound)?;

    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;
    let removed = Repository::delete_user_device(&state.pool, sub.user_id, &payload.device_id).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!({
            "success": removed,
            "message": if removed { "Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø­Ø°Ù Ú¯Ø±Ø¯ÛŒØ¯." } else { "Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡ Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯ Ù†Ø¸Ø± ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯." }
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/client/heartbeat
/// Handles periodic client heartbeats, enforces quotas/device validity, and checks anomalies
async fn client_heartbeat(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
    Json(payload): Json<HeartbeatRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = payload
        .token
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get("X-Subscription-Token")
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .map(|s| s.to_string())
        })
        .or_else(|| {
            headers
                .get(header::AUTHORIZATION)
                .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
                .and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("Bearer "))
                .map(|s| s.replace("jwt_sentra_", ""))
        })
        .ok_or(CoreError::SubscriptionNotFound)?;

    let mode = payload.mode.as_deref().unwrap_or("web_browsing");

    let verdict = Repository::process_heartbeat(
        &state.pool,
        &token,
        &payload.device_id,
        mode,
        payload.delta_upload_bytes,
        payload.delta_download_bytes,
    )
    .await?;

    let delta_total = (payload.delta_upload_bytes + payload.delta_download_bytes) as i64;
    if delta_total > 0 {
        if let Ok(sub) = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await {
            // If free tier user, record traffic usage on active donated outbounds and trigger reward if capacity > 200 GB
            if sub.tier == "Sentra Lite" {
                let _ = Repository::record_donated_outbound_usage(&state.pool, mode, delta_total, state.bot.as_ref()).await;
            }

            let is_anomaly = state
                .anomaly_detector
                .record_and_check(
                    &state.pool,
                    sub.user_id,
                    delta_total,
                    state.bot.as_ref(),
                    state.admin_chat_id,
                )
                .await
                .unwrap_or(false);

            if is_anomaly {
                return Ok((
                    StatusCode::OK,
                    [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
                    Json(json!({
                        "action": "disconnect",
                        "code": "ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED",
                        "message": "Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ú¯ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ØµØ±Ù Ù…Ø´Ú©ÙˆÚ© Ùˆ ØºÛŒØ±Ø¹Ø§Ø¯ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ú¯Ø±Ø¯ÛŒØ¯."
                    })),
                )
                    .into_response());
            }
        }
    }

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!(verdict)),
    )
        .into_response())
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct ClientVersionQuery {
    pub platform: Option<String>,
}

/// GET /api/v1/client/version
/// Returns latest client application version, minimum supported version, and release notes from app_releases
async fn client_version(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Query(query): Query<ClientVersionQuery>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let target_platform = query.platform.as_deref().unwrap_or("windows");
    let release_opt = Repository::get_latest_app_release(&state.pool).await?;

    let (latest_version, release_notes, release_date, file_name, file_size_mb, download_url) = match release_opt {
        Some(rel) => {
            let dl_url = if target_platform == "android" {
                rel.android_github_url.unwrap_or_else(|| "https://t.me/SentraVpnBot".to_string())
            } else {
                rel.windows_github_url.unwrap_or_else(|| "https://t.me/SentraVpnBot".to_string())
            };
            (
                rel.version,
                if rel.changelog.is_empty() {
                    format!("Ø¨Ø±ÙˆØ²Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø±Ø³Ù…ÛŒ Ú©Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ†Øª Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Sentra Connect Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ {}", target_platform)
                } else {
                    rel.changelog
                },
                rel.updated_at.format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string(),
                if target_platform == "android" { "Sentra-v1.0.0-Android.apk".to_string() } else { "Sentra-v1.0.0-Windows-Setup.exe".to_string() },
                if target_platform == "android" { 35.0 } else { 45.0 },
                dl_url,
            )
        }
        None => (
            "1.0.0".to_string(),
            "Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Û±.Û°.Û°: Ø¶Ø±Ø¨Ø§Ù† Ù‚Ù„Ø¨ Ùˆ Ù‡Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ù…â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ù„Ø­Ø¸Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ø­Ø¬Ù…ØŒ Ø§ÛŒØ²ÙˆÙ„Ø§Ø³ÛŒÙˆÙ† Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„Øª Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨ØªØŒ Ù…Ø­Ø§ÙØ¸Øª Ù†Ø´Øª DNSØŒ Ø¨Ø§Ø²ÛŒØ§Ø¨ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø´Ø¨Ú©Ù‡ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯.".to_string(),
            "2026-11-22".to_string(),
            if target_platform == "windows" {
                "Sentra-v1.0.0-Windows-Setup.exe".to_string()
            } else {
                "Sentra-v1.0.0-Android.apk".to_string()
            },
            40.0,
            "https://t.me/SentraVpnBot".to_string(),
        ),
    };

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8")],
        Json(json!({
            "latest_version": latest_version,
            "min_supported_version": "1.0.0",
            "platform": target_platform,
            "file_name": file_name,
            "file_size_mb": file_size_mb,
            "download_url": download_url,
            "release_notes": release_notes,
            "release_date": release_date,
            "mandatory": false
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/v1/client/telemetry
/// Reports traffic usage deltas and evaluates Sliding Window Rate limits (Anomaly Protection)
async fn client_telemetry(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(payload): Json<TelemetryReportRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &payload.token).await?;

    // Check if account is suspended
    let user_opt = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;
    if let Some(user) = user_opt {
        if user.status == "suspended" || sub.status == "suspended" {
            return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended(
                "Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ú¯ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ØµØ±Ù ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ© ØºÛŒØ±Ø¹Ø§Ø¯ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…ØªÙˆÙ‚Ù Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒØ¯.".to_string(),
            ));
        }
    }

    // Update device active timestamp if device_id provided
    if let Some(ref dev_id) = payload.device_id {
        let _ = Repository::register_or_update_device(
            &state.pool,
            sub.user_id,
            sub.id,
            dev_id,
            "Sentra Connected Device",
            "desktop",
        )
        .await;
    }

    // Check sliding window anomaly: >5GB in 15m or >20GB in 1h
    let is_anomaly = state
        .anomaly_detector
        .record_and_check(
            &state.pool,
            sub.user_id,
            payload.bytes_delta,
            state.bot.as_ref(),
            state.admin_chat_id,
        )
        .await?;

    if is_anomaly {
        return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended(
            "Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ú¯ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ØµØ±Ù ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ© ØºÛŒØ±Ø¹Ø§Ø¯ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…ØªÙˆÙ‚Ù Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒØ¯.".to_string(),
        ));
    }

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "success": true,
            "status": "normal"
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// GET /api/v1/sub/:token and GET /sub/:token
/// Returns Base64 V2Ray URI list or Sing-box JSON depending on User-Agent
async fn get_subscription(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(token): Path<String>,
    Query(query): Query<SubQuery>,
    headers: HeaderMap,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;

    // Check suspension
    let user_opt = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;
    if let Some(user) = user_opt {
        if user.status == "suspended" || sub.status == "suspended" {
            return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended(
                "Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ú¯ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ØµØ±Ù ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ© ØºÛŒØ±Ø¹Ø§Ø¯ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…ØªÙˆÙ‚Ù Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒØ¯.".to_string(),
            ));
        }
    }

    if sub.expires_at < Utc::now() {
        return Err(CoreError::SubscriptionExpired(sub.expires_at.to_rfc3339()));
    }

    let user_agent = headers
        .get(header::USER_AGENT)
        .and_then(|h| h.to_str().ok())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .to_lowercase();

    let wants_singbox = query
        .format
        .as_deref()
        .map(|f| f.eq_ignore_ascii_case("singbox") || f.eq_ignore_ascii_case("sing-box"))
        .unwrap_or(false)
        || user_agent.contains("sing-box")
        || user_agent.contains("singbox");

    let outbounds = Repository::get_allowed_outbounds(&state.pool, &sub.tier).await?;

    let sub_userinfo = format!(
        "upload={}; download={}; total={}; expire={}",
        sub.upload_bytes,
        sub.download_bytes,
        sub.traffic_limit_bytes,
        sub.expires_at.timestamp()
    );

    if wants_singbox {
        let singbox_json = compiler::compile_singbox_config(&sub, &outbounds, None);
        Ok((
            StatusCode::OK,
            [
                (header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json; charset=utf-8"),
                (
                    header::HeaderName::from_static("subscription-userinfo"),
                    sub_userinfo.as_str(),
                ),
            ],
            Json(singbox_json),
        )
            .into_response())
    } else {
        // Base64 V2Ray list
        let mut lines = Vec::new();
        for ob in outbounds {
            let uri = match ob.protocol.as_str() {
                "socks" => format!("socks://{}:{}#{}", ob.server_address, ob.server_port, urlencoding_simple(&ob.tag)),
                "http" => format!("http://{}:{}#{}", ob.server_address, ob.server_port, urlencoding_simple(&ob.tag)),
                "vless" => format!(
                    "vless://{}@{}:{}?security=reality&type=tcp#{}",
                    ob.uuid.as_deref().unwrap_or("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"),
                    ob.server_address,
                    ob.server_port,
                    urlencoding_simple(&ob.tag)
                ),
                _ => format!("socks://{}:{}#{}", ob.server_address, ob.server_port, urlencoding_simple(&ob.tag)),
            };
            lines.push(uri);
        }

        let raw_payload = lines.join("\n");
        let encoded_payload = BASE64.encode(raw_payload.as_bytes());

        Ok((
            StatusCode::OK,
            [
                (header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
                (header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION, "attachment; filename=\"sentra-sub.txt\""),
                (
                    header::HeaderName::from_static("subscription-userinfo"),
                    sub_userinfo.as_str(),
                ),
            ],
            encoded_payload,
        )
            .into_response())
    }
}

/// GET /api/v1/ovpn/:token
async fn get_openvpn_profile(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(token): Path<String>,
    Query(query): Query<OvpnQuery>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let mut sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, &token).await?;

    // Check suspension
    let user_opt = Repository::get_user_by_id(&state.pool, sub.user_id).await?;
    if let Some(user) = user_opt {
        if user.status == "suspended" || sub.status == "suspended" {
            return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended(
                "Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ú¯ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ØµØ±Ù ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ© ØºÛŒØ±Ø¹Ø§Ø¯ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…ØªÙˆÙ‚Ù Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒØ¯.".to_string(),
            ));
        }
    }

    // Check expiration
    if sub.expires_at < Utc::now() {
        return Err(CoreError::SubscriptionExpired(sub.expires_at.to_rfc3339()));
    }

    // Register/update device session for iOS OpenVPN (Max 3 Devices)
    let dev_id = format!("ios-ovpn-{}", &sub.token[..8]);
    let _ = Repository::register_or_update_device(
        &state.pool,
        sub.user_id,
        sub.id,
        &dev_id,
        "iPhone OpenVPN",
        "ios",
    )
    .await;

    let mode = query.mode.as_deref().unwrap_or("web_browsing");

    // Validate mode
    if mode == "gaming" {
        return Err(CoreError::ModeComingSoon {
            mode: "Ú¯ÛŒÙ…ÛŒÙ†Ú¯ (Gaming)".to_string(),
        });
    }
    if mode == "static_ip" {
        if sub.tier == "Sentra Lite" {
            return Err(CoreError::ModeLocked {
                mode: "Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª (Static IP)".to_string(),
            });
        }
        if sub.static_ip_used_mb >= 30_720 {
            return Err(CoreError::StaticIpQuotaExceeded {
                used_mb: sub.static_ip_used_mb,
                limit_mb: 30_720,
            });
        }
    }

    // Daily quota check
    Repository::check_and_reset_daily_quota(&state.pool, &mut sub).await?;
    if sub.tier == "Sentra Lite" {
        if mode == "web_browsing" && sub.web_browsing_used_mb >= 2048 {
            return Err(CoreError::DailyQuotaExceeded {
                mode: "ÙˆØ¨â€ŒÚ¯Ø±Ø¯ÛŒ".to_string(),
                used_mb: sub.web_browsing_used_mb,
                limit_mb: 2048,
            });
        } else if mode == "download" && sub.download_used_mb >= 3072 {
            return Err(CoreError::DailyQuotaExceeded {
                mode: "Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯".to_string(),
                used_mb: sub.download_used_mb,
                limit_mb: 3072,
            });
        }
    }

    let raw_protocol = query.protocol.as_deref().unwrap_or("openvpn_udp");
    let proto = match raw_protocol.to_lowercase().as_str() {
        "openvpn_tcp" | "tcp" => "openvpn_tcp",
        "stealth" => "stealth",
        _ => "openvpn_udp",
    };

    let server_host = if state.server_host == "0.0.0.0" || state.server_host.is_empty() {
        "127.0.0.1".to_string()
    } else {
        state.server_host.clone()
    };

    let ovpn_text = compiler::generate_ovpn_profile_ext(&sub, Some(mode), proto, &server_host);

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        [
            (header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/x-openvpn-profile"),
            (
                header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                "attachment; filename=\"sentra-client.ovpn\"",
            ),
        ],
        ovpn_text,
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/internal/openvpn/auth
/// Internal hook for OpenVPN server to authenticate users and enforce static IP 30GB limits
async fn openvpn_internal_auth(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(payload): Json<OpenVpnAuthRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let token = payload.password.as_deref().unwrap_or(&payload.username);
    let sub = Repository::get_subscription_by_token(&state.pool, token).await?;

    let mode = payload.mode.as_deref().unwrap_or("web_browsing");
    if mode == "static_ip" {
        if sub.tier == "Sentra Lite" {
            return Err(CoreError::ModeLocked {
                mode: "Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª (Static IP)".to_string(),
            });
        }
        if sub.static_ip_used_mb >= 30_720 {
            return Err(CoreError::StaticIpQuotaExceeded {
                used_mb: sub.static_ip_used_mb,
                limit_mb: 30_720,
            });
        }
    }

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "status": "ok",
            "user_id": sub.user_id,
            "tier": sub.tier,
            "mode": mode
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// POST /api/internal/openvpn/accounting
/// Internal hook for OpenVPN server to account traffic and enforce static IP disconnects
async fn openvpn_internal_accounting(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(payload): Json<OpenVpnAccountingRequest>,
) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let mode = payload.mode.as_deref().unwrap_or("web_browsing");
    let verdict = Repository::process_heartbeat(
        &state.pool,
        &payload.token,
        "internal-openvpn-session",
        mode,
        payload.bytes_sent,
        payload.bytes_received,
    )
    .await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!(verdict)),
    )
        .into_response())
}

/// GET /health
async fn health_check(State(state): State<AppState>) -> Result<Response, CoreError> {
    let stats = Repository::get_system_health(&state.pool).await?;

    Ok((
        StatusCode::OK,
        Json(json!({
            "status": "healthy",
            "service": "sentra-core",
            "timestamp": Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
            "layers": {
                "l1_database_wal": stats.wal_mode_active,
                "l2_rest_api_port_8080": true,
                "l3_fakeip_dns_pool": "198.18.0.0/15 (ipv4_only)",
                "l4_active_outbounds": stats.active_outbounds,
                "l5_tokio_async_runtime": "operational",
                "l6_telegram_dispatcher": "healthy"
            },
            "metrics": {
                "total_users": stats.total_users,
                "lite_users": stats.lite_users,
                "pro_users": stats.pro_users,
                "flow_users": stats.flow_users,
                "active_outbounds": stats.active_outbounds,
                "open_tickets": stats.open_tickets
            }
        })),
    )
        .into_response())
}

fn urlencoding_simple(input: &str) -> String {
    input.replace(' ', "%20")
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AdminOutboundState {
    AwaitingOutboundInput { category: String },
}

static ADMIN_OUTBOUND_STATES: OnceLock<RwLock<HashMap<i64, AdminOutboundState>>> = OnceLock::new();

fn outbound_states_map() -> &'static RwLock<HashMap<i64, AdminOutboundState>> {
    ADMIN_OUTBOUND_STATES.get_or_init(|| RwLock::new(HashMap::new()))
}

pub async fn set_admin_outbound_state(admin_id: i64, state: AdminOutboundState) {
    let mut map = outbound_states_map().write().await;
    map.insert(admin_id, state);
}

pub async fn get_admin_outbound_state(admin_id: i64) -> Option<AdminOutboundState> {
    let map = outbound_states_map().read().await;
    map.get(&admin_id).cloned()
}

pub async fn clear_admin_outbound_state(admin_id: i64) {
    let mut map = outbound_states_map().write().await;
    map.remove(&admin_id);
}

pub fn is_authorized_admin(telegram_id: i64, admin_chat_id: i64) -> bool {
    admin_chat_id == 0 || telegram_id == admin_chat_id
}

fn format_traffic(bytes: i64) -> String {
    if bytes <= 0 {
        return "0 MB".to_string();
    }
    let mb = bytes as f64 / 1_048_576.0;
    if mb >= 1024.0 {
        format!("{:.2} GB", mb / 1024.0)
    } else {
        format!("{:.1} MB", mb)
    }
}

/// Open Master Admin Panel with glass inline dashboard
pub async fn handle_admin_panel(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_id = msg.from.as_ref().map(|u| u.id.0 as i64).unwrap_or(0);

        r#"ðŸ›¡ <b>Ø¯Ø§Ø´Ø¨ÙˆØ±Ø¯ Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª Ù…Ø±Ú©Ø²ÛŒ Ø³Ù†ØªØ±Ø§ (Sentra Master Admin)</b>

/// 2. Search & User Management handler
pub async fn handle_admin_search_user(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    query_str: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_id = msg.from.as_ref().map(|u| u.id.0 as i64).unwrap_or(0);
    if !is_authorized_admin(telegram_id, admin_chat_id) {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let user_opt = Repository::find_user_by_query(pool, query_str).await?;
    let user = match user_opt {
        Some(u) => u,
        None => {
            bot.send_message(
                msg.chat.id,
                format!("âŒ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ ÛŒØ§ ÛŒÙˆØ²Ø±Ù†ÛŒÙ… <code>{}</code> Ø¯Ø± Ø³Ø§Ù…Ø§Ù†Ù‡ ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.", query_str),
            )
            .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
            .reply_markup(admin_dashboard_inline_keyboard())
            .await?;
            return Ok(());
        }
    };

    render_user_card(bot, msg.chat.id, None, pool, user.id).await?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Render or edit a user card with full details and action buttons

/// Upgrade user subscription to Pro / Flow+ for 30 days

    let alert_msg = format!("âœ… Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú© Ø¨Ù‡ {} Ø§Ø±ØªÙ‚Ø§ ÛŒØ§ÙØª (Ø§Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø§Ø± Û³Û° Ø±ÙˆØ²Ù‡)", sub.tier);

            r#"ðŸŽ‰ <b>Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú© Ø´Ù…Ø§ ØªÙˆØ³Ø· Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª Ø³Ù†ØªØ±Ø§ Ø§Ø±ØªÙ‚Ø§ ÛŒØ§ÙØª!</b>

                .send_message(ChatId(tid), notify_text)

/// Toggle suspension/block status of user

) -> anyhow::Result<()> {

fn extract_amount_from_caption(caption: Option<&str>) -> i64 {
    if let Some(c) = caption {
        let mut digits = String::new();
        for ch in c.chars() {
            if ch.is_ascii_digit() {
                digits.push(ch);
            }
        }
        if let Ok(num) = digits.parse::<i64>() {
            return num;
        }
    }
    0
}

/// Admin approves financial donation

) -> anyhow::Result<()> {

/// Toggle outbound status callback
pub async fn handle_toggle_outbound_callback(
    bot: &Bot,
    q: &CallbackQuery,
    pool: &DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,

) -> anyhow::Result<()> {

        let _ = bot.answer_callback_query(q.id.clone()).text("â›” Ø¹Ø¯Ù… Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ").await;
        return Ok(());

    let status_str = if is_now_active { "ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯ ðŸŸ¢" } else { "ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯ ðŸ”´" };

            .edit_message_reply_markup(msg.chat().id, msg.id())
            .reply_markup(admin_outbounds_inline_keyboard(&outbounds))
            .await;
    }

/// Admin sends version & changelog text
pub async fn handle_admin_version_text(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    platform: &str,
    text: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_user_id = msg.from.as_ref().map(|u| u.id.0 as i64).unwrap_or(0);
    if !is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {
        return Ok(());
    }

    if text == "/cancel" {
        clear_admin_upload_state(telegram_user_id).await;
        bot.send_message(msg.chat.id, "âŒ ÙØ±Ø¢ÛŒÙ†Ø¯ Ø¢Ù¾Ù„ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ù„ØºÙˆ Ø´Ø¯.")
            .reply_markup(admin_dashboard_inline_keyboard())
            .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }

    let (version, changelog) = if let Some(dash_idx) = text.find('-') {
        (
            text[..dash_idx].trim().to_string(),
            text[dash_idx + 1..].trim().to_string(),
        )
    } else if let Some(colon_idx) = text.find(':') {
        (
            text[..colon_idx].trim().to_string(),
            text[colon_idx + 1..].trim().to_string(),
        )
    } else {
        let mut parts = text.split_whitespace();
        let v = parts.next().unwrap_or("1.0.0").to_string();
        let rest: Vec<&str> = parts.collect();
        let c = if rest.is_empty() {
            format!("Ø§Ù†ØªØ´Ø§Ø± Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ {}", v)
        } else {
            rest.join(" ")
        };
        (v, c)
    };

    set_admin_upload_state(
        telegram_user_id,
        AdminUploadState::AwaitingFile {
            platform: platform.to_string(),
            version: version.clone(),
            changelog: changelog.clone(),
        },
    )
    .await;

    let (platform_fa, ext_hint) = if platform == "windows" {
        ("ÙˆÛŒÙ†Ø¯ÙˆØ² (Windows)", "<code>.exe</code> ÛŒØ§ <code>.msi</code>")
    } else {
        ("Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯ (Android)", "<code>.apk</code>")
    };

    let reply = format!(
        r#"âœ… <b>Ù…Ø´Ø®ØµØ§Øª Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ø´Ø¯:</b>
â€¢ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ…â€ŒØ¹Ø§Ù…Ù„: <b>{}</b>
â€¢ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ±Ø§Øª: <i>{}</i>

ðŸ“ <b>Ù…Ø±Ø­Ù„Ù‡ Û² Ø§Ø² Û²:</b>
Ø§Ú©Ù†ÙˆÙ† Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ <b>ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù†ØµØ¨ÛŒ ({})</b> Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª <b>Document (ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù¾ÛŒÙˆØ³Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù…)</b> Ø¯Ø± Ù‡Ù…ÛŒÙ† Ú†Øª Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ ÙØ±Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.

<i>(Ø¬Ù‡Øª Ø§Ù†ØµØ±Ø§Ù Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù†Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯: /cancel)</i>"#,
        platform_fa, version, changelog, ext_hint
    );

    bot.send_message(msg.chat.id, reply)
        .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
        .await?;

    Ok(())
}

/// Admin uploads Document installer file
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub async fn handle_admin_file_upload(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    doc: &teloxide::types::Document,
    platform: &str,
    version: &str,
    changelog: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_user_id = msg.from.as_ref().map(|u| u.id.0 as i64).unwrap_or(0);
    if !is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let default_name = format!(
        "SentraConnect-{}.{}",
        version,
        if platform == "windows" { "exe" } else { "apk" }
    );
    let file_name = doc.file_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(&default_name);
    let lower_name = file_name.to_lowercase();

    // Validate extension
    let is_valid_ext = if platform == "windows" {
        lower_name.ends_with(".exe") || lower_name.ends_with(".msi")
    } else if platform == "android" {
        lower_name.ends_with(".apk")
    } else {
        false
    };

    if !is_valid_ext {
        let expected = if platform == "windows" {
            "<code>.exe</code> ÛŒØ§ <code>.msi</code>"
        } else {
            "<code>.apk</code>"
        };
        let err_msg = format!(
            "âŒ <b>Ù¾Ø³ÙˆÙ†Ø¯ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª!</b>\n\nØ¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ {}ØŒ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø³ÙˆÙ†Ø¯ {} Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.\nÙ„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù†ØµØ¨ÛŒ ØµØ­ÛŒØ­ Ø±Ø§ Ø¨ÙØ±Ø³ØªÛŒØ¯ ÛŒØ§ /cancel Ø±Ø§ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯.",
            if platform == "windows" { "ÙˆÛŒÙ†Ø¯ÙˆØ²" } else { "Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯" },
            expected
        );
        bot.send_message(msg.chat.id, err_msg)
            .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
            .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }

    let size_bytes = doc.file.size as f64;
    let file_size_mb = (size_bytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0) * 100.0).round() / 100.0;
    let file_id = doc.file.id.clone();

    // Save into database
    Repository::upsert_app_release(
        pool,
        platform,
        version,
        &file_id,
        file_name,
        file_size_mb,
        changelog,
    )
    .await?;

    // Clear admin upload state
    clear_admin_upload_state(telegram_user_id).await;

    let success_text = format!(
        r#"ðŸŽ‰ <b>Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ù¾Ù„ÛŒÚ©ÛŒØ´Ù† Sentra Connect Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ù…Ù†ØªØ´Ø± Ø´Ø¯!</b>

ðŸ“Œ <b>Ù…Ø´Ø®ØµØ§Øª Ø§Ù†ØªØ´Ø§Ø±:</b>
â€¢ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ…â€ŒØ¹Ø§Ù…Ù„: <b>{}</b>
â€¢ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ Ù†Ø§Ù… ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ Ø­Ø¬Ù… ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„: <b>{:.2} Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª</b>
â€¢ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… (File ID):
<code>{}</code>

ðŸ“ <b>ØªÙˆØ¶ÛŒØ­Ø§Øª ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ±Ø§Øª:</b>
<i>{}</i>

âœ¨ Ú©Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ†Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯Ø³Ú©ØªØ§Ù¾ Ùˆ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±Ø§Ù† Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª Ù‡Ù…â€ŒØ§Ú©Ù†ÙˆÙ† Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†Ù†Ø¯ Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø±Ø§ Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ù…Ø§ÛŒÙ†Ø¯."#,
        if platform == "windows" { "ÙˆÛŒÙ†Ø¯ÙˆØ² (Windows)" } else { "Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯ (Android)" },
        version,
        file_name,
        file_size_mb,
        file_id,
        changelog
    );

    bot.send_message(msg.chat.id, success_text)
        .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
        .reply_markup(admin_dashboard_inline_keyboard())
        .await?;

    Ok(())
}

/// Set outbound allocation mode (free_only, static_only, both, auto)
pub async fn handle_set_outbound_allocation_callback(
    bot: &Bot,
    q: &CallbackQuery,
    pool: &DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    outbound_id: i64,
    alloc_mode: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_user_id = q.from.id.0 as i64;
    if !is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {
        bot.answer_callback_query(q.id.clone()).text("â›” Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ ØºÛŒØ±Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø²!").show_alert(true).await?;
        return Ok(());
    }

        "free_only" => (true, true, false),
        "static_only" => (false, false, true),
        "both" => (true, true, true),
        _ => (true, true, false), // auto initially starts with free, updated by engine
    };

    Repository::update_outbound_allocation(
        pool,
        outbound_id,
        allow_web,
        allow_download,
        allow_static_ip,
        alloc_mode,
    )
    .await?;

    // Auto-sync server bridge
    let _ = crate::bridge_sync::sync_server_bridge_config(pool).await;

    let desc = match alloc_mode {
        "free_only" => "ðŸŒ ÙÙ‚Ø· Ø¨Ø®Ø´ Ø±Ø§ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ù† (ÙˆØ¨ / Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯)",
        "static_only" => "ðŸ’Ž ÙÙ‚Ø· Ø¨Ø®Ø´ Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª (ØªØ±ÛŒØ¯ Ùˆ Ø¬Ù…Ù†Ø§ÛŒ)",
        "both" => "âš¡ï¸ Ù‡Ø± Ø¯Ùˆ Ø¨Ø®Ø´ (Ø±Ø§ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ù† + Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª)",
        _ => "ðŸ¤– ØªØ´Ø®ÛŒØµ Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø±",
    };

    bot.answer_callback_query(q.id.clone())
        .text(format!("âœ… ØªØ®ØµÛŒØµ Ù†ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ [{}] ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± ÛŒØ§ÙØª.", desc))
        .show_alert(true)
        .await?;

            msg.chat().id,
            msg.id(),
            format!("âœ… <b>ØªØ®ØµÛŒØµ Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ Ù†ÙˆØ¯ #{} Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø±ÙˆÛŒ [{}] ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ… Ùˆ Ø¯Ø± Ù¾Ù„ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ù‡Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ù… Ø´Ø¯.</b>", outbound_id, desc),

        .reply_markup(admin_official_outbounds_hub_keyboard())
        .await;
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Inline keyboard for downloading the Sentra Connect client apps
pub fn download_app_inline_keyboard(release: Option<&AppRelease>) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup {
    let github_url = release
        .and_then(|r| {
            if let Some(ref w) = r.windows_github_url {
                Some(w.as_str())
            } else if let Some(ref a) = r.android_github_url {
                Some(a.as_str())
            } else {
                None
            }
        })
        .unwrap_or("https://github.com/sentravpn/client/releases/latest");

    let valid_url = url::Url::parse(github_url)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| url::Url::parse("https://github.com").unwrap());

    InlineKeyboardMarkup::new(vec![
        vec![
            InlineKeyboardButton::callback("ðŸ–¥ Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ ÙˆÛŒÙ†Ø¯ÙˆØ² (.exe)", "dl_app_windows"),
            InlineKeyboardButton::callback("ðŸ“± Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯ (.apk)", "dl_app_android"),
        ],
        vec![
            InlineKeyboardButton::url("ðŸ”— ØµÙØ­Ù‡ Ø§Ù†ØªØ´Ø§Ø± Ú¯ÛŒØªÙ‡Ø§Ø¨", valid_url),
        ],
    ])
}

/// Inline keyboard for toggling outbounds in Admin panel
pub fn admin_outbounds_inline_keyboard(outbounds: &[Outbound]) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup {
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    for ob in outbounds {
        let status_icon = if ob.is_active == 1 { "ðŸŸ¢ ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„" } else { "ðŸ”´ ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„" };
        let button_text = format!("{} | {} ({})", status_icon, ob.tag, ob.min_tier);
        let callback_data = format!("toggle_ob_{}", ob.id);
        rows.push(vec![InlineKeyboardButton::callback(button_text, callback_data)]);
    }
    InlineKeyboardMarkup::new(rows)
}

=== FILE: sentra-core\src\bot\mod.rs ===
pub mod admin_handlers;
pub mod keyboards;
pub mod rate_limiter;
pub mod user_handlers;

use crate::db::DbPool;
use std::sync::Arc;
use teloxide::prelude::*;
use teloxide::types::ParseMode;
use tracing::{error, info, warn};

pub async fn run_telegram_bot(
    token_opt: Option<String>,
    proxy_opt: Option<String>,
    pool: DbPool,
    admin_chat_id: i64,
    base_url: String,
    pro_subscription_price: i64,
) {
    let token = match token_opt {
        Some(t) if !t.trim().is_empty() && !t.contains("SAMPLE") => t,
        _ => {
            warn!("âš ï¸ TELOXIDE_TOKEN is not configured or is a placeholder. Telegram Bot long-polling daemon skipped, Axum REST API active.");
            return;
        }
    };

    // Initialize bot client with optional proxy (TELOXIDE_PROXY)
    let bot = if let Some(ref proxy_url) = proxy_opt {
        info!("Connecting Telegram Bot through proxy: {}", proxy_url);
        match reqwest::Proxy::all(proxy_url) {
            Ok(proxy) => {
                match teloxide::net::default_reqwest_settings().proxy(proxy).build() {
                    Ok(client) => Bot::with_client(token, client),
                    Err(e) => {
                        error!("Failed to build reqwest client with proxy: {}. Falling back to direct connection.", e);
                        Bot::new(token)
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Invalid proxy URL format ({}): {}. Falling back to direct.", proxy_url, e);
                Bot::new(token)
            }
        }
    } else {
        Bot::new(token)
    };

    info!("Starting Sentra Telegram Bot in parallel Long Polling mode...");

    let pool = Arc::new(pool);
    let base_url = Arc::new(base_url);
    let rate_limiter = Arc::new(rate_limiter::BotRateLimiter::new());

    let handler = dptree::entry()
        .branch(
            Update::filter_message().endpoint({
                let pool = Arc::clone(&pool);
                let base_url = Arc::clone(&base_url);
                let rate_limiter = Arc::clone(&rate_limiter);
                move |bot: Bot, msg: Message| {
                    let pool = Arc::clone(&pool);
                    let base_url = Arc::clone(&base_url);
                    let rate_limiter = Arc::clone(&rate_limiter);
                    async move {
                        // Anti-spam / Flood Protection (> 5 reqs in < 10s -> 5m Cooldown)
                        let telegram_user_id = msg.from.as_ref().map(|u| u.id.0 as i64).unwrap_or(0);
                        if telegram_user_id != 0 {
                            match rate_limiter.check(telegram_user_id).await {
                                rate_limiter::RateLimitVerdict::BlockAndWarn => {
                                    let _ = bot.send_message(
                                        msg.chat.id,
                                        "â›” Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø¯Ø±Ø®ÙˆØ§Ø³Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾ÛŒâ€ŒØ¯Ø±Ù¾ÛŒØŒ Ø¯Ø³ØªØ±Ø³ÛŒ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¯Øª Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡ Ù…Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯.",
                                    )
                                    .await;
                                    return respond(());
                                }
                                rate_limiter::RateLimitVerdict::SilentDrop => {
                                    return respond(());
                                }
                                rate_limiter::RateLimitVerdict::Allowed => {}
                            }
                        }

                        // 1. Check if user sent a Photo (e.g. manual payment receipt or broadcast with photo)
                        if let Some(photos) = msg.photo() {
                            if !photos.is_empty() {
                                // If admin sending a photo with caption starting with /broadcast
                                if admin_handlers::is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {
                                    if let Some(caption) = msg.caption() {
                                        if let Some(stripped) = caption.strip_prefix("/broadcast ") {
                                            let broadcast_text = stripped.trim();
                                            let best_photo = &photos[photos.len() - 1];
                                            if let Err(e) = admin_handlers::handle_admin_broadcast(
                                                &bot,
                                                &msg,
                                                &pool,
                                                admin_chat_id,
                                                broadcast_text,
                                                Some(best_photo.file.id.clone()),
                                            )
                                            .await
                                            {
                                                error!("Error in broadcast with photo: {:?}", e);
                                            }
                                            return respond(());
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                // Regular user or payment / donation receipt submission
                                if let Err(e) = admin_handlers::handle_receipt_submission(&bot, &msg, &pool, admin_chat_id, pro_subscription_price).await {
                                    error!("Error in handle_receipt_submission: {:?}", e);
                                }
                                return respond(());
                            }
                        }

                        // 1.5. Check if user sent a Document (e.g. app release upload by admin)
                        if let Some(doc) = msg.document() {
                            if admin_handlers::is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {
                                if let Some(upload_state) = admin_handlers::get_admin_upload_state(telegram_user_id).await {
                                    match upload_state {
                                        admin_handlers::AdminUploadState::AwaitingFile { platform, version, changelog } => {
                                            if let Err(e) = admin_handlers::handle_admin_file_upload(
                                                &bot,
                                                &msg,
                                                &pool,
                                                admin_chat_id,
                                                doc,
                                                &platform,
                                                &version,
                                                &changelog,
                                            )
                                            .await
                                            {
                                                error!("Error in handle_admin_file_upload: {:?}", e);
                                            }
                                            return respond(());
                                        }
                                        admin_handlers::AdminUploadState::AwaitingVersionAndChangelog { .. } => {

                                                msg.chat.id,
                                                "âš ï¸ Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø§Ø¨ØªØ¯Ø§ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ùˆ ØªÙˆØ¶ÛŒØ­ Ú©ÙˆØªØ§Ù‡ ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ±Ø§Øª Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ù…ØªÙ† Ø¨ÙØ±Ø³ØªÛŒØ¯ØŒ Ø³Ù¾Ø³ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø±Ø§ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù†Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.",

                                            return respond(());
                                        }

                                                msg.chat.id,
                                                "âš ï¸ Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ø¨Ø®Ø´ Ù…Ù†ØªØ¸Ø± Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú© Release Ø¯Ø± Ú¯ÛŒØªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§Ø¨ Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ù…ØªÙ†ÛŒ Ø§Ø³ØªØŒ Ù†Ù‡ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù†ØµØ¨ÛŒ.",
                                            ).await;
                                            return respond(());
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // 2. Text Message Routing
                        if let Some(text) = msg.text() {
                            let text = text.trim();

                            // Cancel command check in text
                            if text == "/cancel" || text == "Ø§Ù†ØµØ±Ø§Ù" || text == "âŒ Ø§Ù†ØµØ±Ø§Ù" {
                                admin_handlers::clear_all_admin_states(telegram_user_id).await;
                                user_handlers::clear_user_state(telegram_user_id).await;

                                    .await;
                                return respond(());
                            }

                            // A. Check active admin state for app release input
                            if admin_handlers::is_authorized_admin(telegram_user_id, admin_chat_id) {

=== FILE: sentra-core\src\bot\rate_limiter.rs ===
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::sync::RwLock;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RateLimitVerdict {
    Allowed,
    BlockAndWarn,
    SilentDrop,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct UserRateEntry {
    timestamps: Vec<Instant>,
    cooldown_until: Option<Instant>,
    warned: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct BotRateLimiter {
    states: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<i64, UserRateEntry>>>,
}

impl BotRateLimiter {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            states: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Check incoming message or button click from a telegram_user_id:
    /// - Max 5 requests in <= 10 seconds.
    /// - Exceeding triggers 5-minute (300s) cooldown.
    /// - Warns user exactly once, then silently drops all updates.
    pub async fn check(&self, telegram_user_id: i64) -> RateLimitVerdict {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let ten_secs = Duration::from_secs(10);
        let cooldown_dur = Duration::from_secs(300);

        let mut map = self.states.write().await;
        let entry = map.entry(telegram_user_id).or_insert_with(|| UserRateEntry {
            timestamps: Vec::new(),
            cooldown_until: None,
            warned: false,
        });

        // 1. Check if currently in Cooldown
        if let Some(until) = entry.cooldown_until {
            if now < until {
                if !entry.warned {
                    entry.warned = true;
                    return RateLimitVerdict::BlockAndWarn;
                } else {
                    return RateLimitVerdict::SilentDrop;
                }
            } else {
                // Cooldown period expired! Reset state
                entry.cooldown_until = None;
                entry.warned = false;
                entry.timestamps.clear();
            }
        }

        // 2. Add current timestamp & keep only timestamps within the last 10 seconds
        entry.timestamps.push(now);
        entry.timestamps.retain(|t| now.duration_since(*t) <= ten_secs);

        // 3. Check threshold: more than 5 requests in <= 10 seconds
        if entry.timestamps.len() > 5 {
            entry.cooldown_until = Some(now + cooldown_dur);
            entry.warned = true;
            return RateLimitVerdict::BlockAndWarn;
        }

        RateLimitVerdict::Allowed
    }
}

use crate::bot::keyboards::{
    download_app_inline_keyboard, ios_modes_inline_keyboard, ios_protocol_choice_keyboard,
    subscription_status_inline_keyboard, upgrade_plans_inline_keyboard,
    user_devices_inline_keyboard, user_main_keyboard, user_main_keyboard_with_admin,
};
use crate::compiler;
use crate::db::{DbPool, Repository};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::OnceLock;
use teloxide::prelude::*;
use teloxide::types::{InputFile, ParseMode};
use tokio::sync::RwLock;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum UserState {
    AwaitingVerifyCode,
    AwaitingDonationConfig,
}

static USER_STATES: OnceLock<RwLock<HashMap<i64, UserState>>> = OnceLock::new();

fn user_states_map() -> &'static RwLock<HashMap<i64, UserState>> {
    USER_STATES.get_or_init(|| RwLock::new(HashMap::new()))
}

pub async fn set_user_state(user_id: i64, state: UserState) {
    let mut map = user_states_map().write().await;
    map.insert(user_id, state);
}

pub async fn get_user_state(user_id: i64) -> Option<UserState> {
    let map = user_states_map().read().await;
    map.get(&user_id).cloned()
}

pub async fn clear_user_state(user_id: i64) {
    let mut map = user_states_map().write().await;
    map.remove(&user_id);
}

        &telegram_user.first_name,
        telegram_user.last_name.as_deref(),
    )

    let code = Repository::generate_auth_code(pool, user.id).await?;

pub async fn handle_subscription_status(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_user = match msg.from.as_ref() {
        Some(u) => u,
        None => return Ok(()),
    };

    let user = Repository::get_or_create_user(
        pool,
        telegram_user.id.0 as i64,
        telegram_user.username.as_deref(),
        &telegram_user.first_name,
        telegram_user.last_name.as_deref(),
    )
    .await?;

    let mut sub = Repository::create_or_ensure_lite_subscription(pool, user.id).await?;
    let _ = Repository::check_and_reset_daily_quota(pool, &mut sub).await;
    let devices = Repository::get_user_devices(pool, user.id).await?;

    let is_pro = sub.tier != "Sentra Lite";
    let static_limit_gb = 30.0;
    let static_used_gb = sub.static_ip_used_mb as f64 / 1024.0;
    let static_rem_gb = (static_limit_gb - static_used_gb).max(0.0);

    let quota_details = if is_pro {
        format!(
            r#"ðŸŒ <b>Ø³Ù‡Ù…ÛŒÙ‡ ÙˆØ¨â€ŒÚ¯Ø±Ø¯ÛŒ:</b> <code>Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ (Unlimited)</code>

ðŸ”’ <b>Ø³Ù‡Ù…ÛŒÙ‡ Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª (ØªØ±ÛŒØ¯/Ø¬Ù…Ù†Ø§ÛŒ):</b> <b>{:.1} GB Ø¨Ø§Ù‚ÛŒÙ…Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø² Û³Û° GB</b> (Ù…ØµØ±Ùâ€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡: {:.1} GB)"#,
            static_rem_gb, static_used_gb

        let dl_rem = (3072 - sub.download_used_mb).max(0) as f64 / 1024.0;

ðŸ”’ <b>Ø¢ÛŒâ€ŒÙ¾ÛŒ Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª:</b> ðŸ”’ Ù‚ÙÙ„ (Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø²Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Ø§Ø±ØªÙ‚Ø§ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú© Pro)"#,

/// Handle incoming proxy link or JSON donation submission
pub async fn handle_donation_submission(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
    raw_text: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let telegram_user = match msg.from.as_ref() {
        Some(u) => u,
        None => return Ok(()),
    };

    let user = Repository::get_or_create_user(
        pool,
        telegram_user.id.0 as i64,
        telegram_user.username.as_deref(),
        &telegram_user.first_name,
        telegram_user.last_name.as_deref(),
    )
    .await?;

    let queue_msg = r#"â³ <b>Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ø±Ø²ÛŒØ§Ø¨ÛŒ Ùˆ Ø¨Ù†Ú†Ù…Ø§Ø±Ú© Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø§Ù‡Ø¯Ø§ÛŒÛŒ...</b>

Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø¯Ø± ØµÙ Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ (Anti-SSRF)ØŒ ØªØ³Øª ØªØ§Ø®ÛŒØ± Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ (Handshake Latency) Ùˆ Ø¨Ù†Ú†Ù…Ø§Ø±Ú© Ø³Ø±Ø¹Øª Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±ÙØª.
Ù†ØªÛŒØ¬Ù‡ Ø§Ø±Ø²ÛŒØ§Ø¨ÛŒ Ùˆ Ù¾Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ø´ Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú© Ø¸Ø±Ù Ú†Ù†Ø¯ Ø«Ø§Ù†ÛŒÙ‡ Ø¢ÛŒÙ†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø®ÙˆØ§Ù‡Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯."#;

    bot.send_message(msg.chat.id, queue_msg)
        .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
        .await?;

    let pool_clone = pool.clone();
    let bot_clone = bot.clone();
    let raw_clone = raw_text.to_string();
    let user_id = user.id;
    let tid = telegram_user.id.0 as i64;

    tokio::spawn(async move {
        crate::outbound_analyzer::OutboundAnalyzer::process_donation(
            pool_clone,
            bot_clone,
            Some(user_id),
            Some(tid),
            raw_clone,
        )
        .await;
    });

    Ok(())
}

/// Handle /link command with or without 6-digit code
pub async fn handle_link_command(
    bot: &Bot,
    msg: &Message,
    pool: &DbPool,
    args: &str,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {

        Some(u) => u,
        None => return Ok(()),
    };

        // Issue new link code for this user
        let user = Repository::get_or_create_user(
            pool,
            tid,
            telegram_user.username.as_deref(),
            &telegram_user.first_name,
            telegram_user.last_name.as_deref(),

/// Handle /setpassword <username> <password> command

/// Handle iOS selection from download menu (connects to OpenVPN iOS guide)
