        let user_uuid = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let display_name = first_name.unwrap_or(clean_username.as_str());

        let user = sqlx::query_as::<_, User>(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO users (user_uuid, username, password_hash, first_name, role, status)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'user', 'active')
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(user_uuid)
        .bind(&clean_username)
        .bind(password_hash)
        .bind(display_name)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        let sub = Self::create_or_ensure_lite_subscription(pool, user.id).await?;
        Ok((user, sub))
    }

    /// Authenticate standalone user with username and password
    pub async fn login_user_with_credentials(
        pool: &DbPool,
        username: &str,
        password: &str,
    ) -> Result<(User, Subscription), CoreError> {
        let clean_username = username.trim().to_lowercase();
        let user_opt = sqlx::query_as::<_, User>(
            "SELECT * FROM users WHERE LOWER(username) = LOWER(?) LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(&clean_username)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        let user = match user_opt {
            Some(u) => u,
            None => return Err(CoreError::AuthFailed("Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string())),
        };

        let hash = match &user.password_hash {
            Some(h) if !h.is_empty() => h,
            _ => return Err(CoreError::AuthFailed("Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø«Ø¨Øª Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø§Ø² Ø·Ø±ÛŒÙ‚ Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… ÛŒØ§ Ú©Ø¯ ÛŒÚ©â€ŒØ¨Ø§Ø± Ù…ØµØ±Ù ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ø´ÙˆÛŒØ¯.".to_string())),
        };

        let valid = Self::verify_password_hash(password, hash);

        if !valid {
            return Err(CoreError::AuthFailed("Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()));
        }

        if user.status == "suspended" {
            return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended("Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙ‚ØªØ§Ù‹ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø· Ø¨Ø§Ø´ÛŒØ¯.".to_string()));
        }
        if user.status == "banned" {
            return Err(CoreError::AuthFailed("Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()));
        }

        let mut sub = Self::create_or_ensure_lite_subscription(pool, user.id).await?;
        Self::check_and_reset_daily_quota(pool, &mut sub).await?;

        Ok((user, sub))
    }

    /// Generate 6-digit temporary account linking code (valid for 5 minutes)
    pub async fn generate_link_code(pool: &DbPool, user_id: i64) -> Result<String, CoreError> {
        let code: u32 = rand::thread_rng().gen_range(100_000..999_999);
        let link_code = format!("{:06}", code);

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE users SET link_code = ?, link_code_expires_at = datetime('now', '+300 seconds') WHERE id = ?"
        )
        .bind(&link_code)
        .bind(user_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(link_code)
    }

    /// Link Telegram account to app account using 6-digit link code
    /// Hardened: checks neither account is suspended, takes MAX of quotas (no quota summing), enforces MAX 3 devices
    pub async fn link_telegram_account_by_code(
        pool: &DbPool,
        telegram_id: i64,
        link_code: &str,
        first_name: &str,
        tg_username: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<User, CoreError> {
        let clean_code = link_code.trim();
        let target_user_opt = sqlx::query_as::<_, User>(
            "SELECT * FROM users WHERE link_code = ? AND link_code_expires_at > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(clean_code)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        let target_user = match target_user_opt {
            Some(u) => u,
            None => return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ú©Ø¯ Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª ÛŒØ§ Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡ Ø§Ø² Ø²Ù…Ø§Ù† ØµØ¯ÙˆØ± Ø¢Ù† Ú¯Ø°Ø´ØªÙ‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string())),
        };

        if target_user.status == "suspended" || target_user.status == "banned" {
            return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended("Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø§Ù¾Ù„ÛŒÚ©ÛŒØ´Ù† Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ ÛŒØ§ ØªØ¹Ù„ÛŒÙ‚ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª Ùˆ Ø§Ù…Ú©Ø§Ù† Ø§Ø¯ØºØ§Ù… Ø¢Ù† ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯.".to_string()));
        }

            if existing_tid != telegram_id {
                return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø±ÛŒ Ù…ØªØµÙ„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()));
            }
        }

        let existing_tg_user = sqlx::query_as::<_, User>(
            "SELECT * FROM users WHERE telegram_id = ? AND id != ? LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(telegram_id)
        .bind(target_user.id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        if let Some(old_u) = existing_tg_user {
            // Security check: Old user must not be suspended or banned
            if old_u.status == "suspended" || old_u.status == "banned" {
                return Err(CoreError::AccountSuspended("Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ ÛŒØ§ ØªØ¹Ù„ÛŒÙ‚ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª Ùˆ Ø§Ù…Ú©Ø§Ù† Ø§Ø¯ØºØ§Ù… Ø¢Ù† ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯.".to_string()));
            }

            let target_sub = Self::get_subscription_by_user_id(pool, target_user.id).await.ok().flatten();

            if let (Some(os), Some(ts)) = (old_sub, target_sub) {
                // Tier resolution: highest tier preserved
                let higher_tier = if os.tier.contains("Flow") || ts.tier.contains("Flow") {
                    "Sentra Flow+"
                } else if os.tier.contains("Pro") || ts.tier.contains("Pro") {
                    "Sentra Pro"
                } else {
                    "Sentra Lite"
                };

                let higher_limit = os.traffic_limit_bytes.max(ts.traffic_limit_bytes);
                let higher_devices = os.max_concurrent_devices.max(ts.max_concurrent_devices).min(3);
                let later_expires = os.expires_at.max(ts.expires_at);

                // Anti-fraud: Quotas MUST NOT be summed! Take MAX(old_used, target_used)
                let max_web_mb = os.web_browsing_used_mb.max(ts.web_browsing_used_mb);
                let max_dl_mb = os.download_used_mb.max(ts.download_used_mb);
                let max_up_bytes = os.upload_bytes.max(ts.upload_bytes);
                let max_down_bytes = os.download_bytes.max(ts.download_bytes);

                // Update target_sub with the reconciled parameters
                sqlx::query(
                    r#"
                    UPDATE subscriptions
                    SET tier = ?,
                        traffic_limit_bytes = ?,
                        max_concurrent_devices = ?,
                        expires_at = ?,
                        web_browsing_used_mb = ?,
                        download_used_mb = ?,
                        upload_bytes = ?,
                        download_bytes = ?,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                    WHERE id = ?
                    "#
                )
                .bind(higher_tier)
                .bind(higher_limit)
                .bind(higher_devices)
                .bind(later_expires)
                .bind(max_web_mb)
                .bind(max_dl_mb)
                .bind(max_up_bytes)
                .bind(max_down_bytes)
                .bind(ts.id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await?;

                // Delete old subscription
                let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM subscriptions WHERE id = ?").bind(os.id).execute(pool).await;

                let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE user_devices SET user_id = ?, subscription_id = ? WHERE user_id = ?")
                    .bind(target_user.id)
                    .bind(ts.id)
                    .bind(old_u.id)
                    .execute(pool)
                    .await;

                // Enforce strictly MAX 3 DEVICES (keep 3 most recent, delete excess)
                #[derive(sqlx::FromRow)]
                struct DevRow {
                    id: i64,
                }
                let user_devs: Vec<DevRow> = sqlx::query_as(
                    "SELECT id FROM user_devices WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY last_active_at DESC"
                )
                .bind(target_user.id)
                .fetch_all(pool)
                .await

                    for dev in &user_devs[3..] {
                        let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM user_devices WHERE id = ?")
                            .bind(dev.id)
                            .execute(pool)
                            .await;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Transfer donations and outbounds to the unified user
            let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE donations SET user_id = ? WHERE user_id = ?")
                .bind(target_user.id)
                .bind(old_u.id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await;

            let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE donated_outbounds SET donor_user_id = ? WHERE donor_user_id = ?")
                .bind(target_user.id)
                .bind(old_u.id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await;

            let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?")
                .bind(old_u.id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await;
        }

        let updated_user = sqlx::query_as::<_, User>(
            r#"
            UPDATE users 
            SET telegram_id = ?, 
                first_name = COALESCE(NULLIF(first_name, 'User'), ?),
                username = COALESCE(username, ?),
                link_code = NULL, 
                link_code_expires_at = NULL,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ?
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(telegram_id)
        .bind(first_name)
        .bind(tg_username)
        .bind(target_user.id)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(updated_user)
    }

    /// Set or update username and password for a user
    pub async fn set_credentials_for_user(
        pool: &DbPool,
        user_id: i64,
        username: &str,
        password: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let clean_username = username.trim().to_lowercase();
        if clean_username.len() < 3 || clean_username.len() > 32 {
            return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨ÛŒÙ† Û³ ØªØ§ Û³Û² Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø§Ú©ØªØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.".to_string()));
        }
        if !clean_username.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_') {
            return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ ÙÙ‚Ø· Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆØ§Ù†Ø¯ Ø´Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø­Ø±ÙˆÙ Ùˆ Ø§Ø¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ Ø§Ù†Ú¯Ù„ÛŒØ³ÛŒ Ùˆ Ø®Ø· Ø²ÛŒØ± (_) Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.".to_string()));
        }
        if Self::RESERVED_USERNAMES.contains(&clean_username.as_str()) {
            return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ø±Ø²Ø±Ùˆ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ùˆ Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„ Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª.".to_string()));
        }
        if password.len() < 8 {
            return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ø±Ù…Ø² Ø¹Ø¨ÙˆØ± Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ù‚Ù„ Û¸ Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø§Ú©ØªØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯.".to_string()));
        }

        let exists: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM users WHERE LOWER(username) = LOWER(?) AND id != ? LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(&clean_username)
        .bind(user_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        if exists.is_some() {
            return Err(CoreError::BadRequest("Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string()));
        }

        let password_hash = Self::hash_password_argon2(password)?;

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE users SET username = ?, password_hash = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?"
        )
        .bind(clean_username)
        .bind(password_hash)
        .bind(user_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Community Outbound Donation Methods
    // =========================================================================

    /// Check if an outbound with same fingerprint hash already exists in system
    pub async fn is_fingerprint_duplicate(
        pool: &DbPool,
        fingerprint: &str,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        let exists: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM donated_outbounds WHERE fingerprint_sha256 = ? AND status != 'rejected' LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(fingerprint)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(exists.is_some())
    }

    /// Check if a user is banned from outbound donation rewards
    pub async fn is_user_reward_banned(pool: &DbPool, user_id: i64) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        let banned: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT outbound_reward_banned FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(user_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(banned.unwrap_or(0) == 1)
    }

    /// Check if an outbound with same server and port already exists in system
    pub async fn is_outbound_duplicate(
        pool: &DbPool,
        server_address: &str,
        server_port: i64,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        let exists_official: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM outbounds WHERE LOWER(server_address) = LOWER(?) AND server_port = ? LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(server_address)
        .bind(server_port)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        if exists_official.is_some() {
            return Ok(true);
        }

        let exists_donated: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM donated_outbounds WHERE LOWER(server_address) = LOWER(?) AND server_port = ? AND status != 'rejected' LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(server_address)
        .bind(server_port)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(exists_donated.is_some())
    }

    /// Save a newly analyzed donated outbound with deep SHA-256 fingerprint
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn save_donated_outbound(
        pool: &DbPool,
        donor_user_id: Option<i64>,
        tag: &str,
        protocol: &str,
        server_address: &str,
        server_port: i64,
        config_json: &str,
        latency_ms: i64,
        speed_mbps: f64,
        assigned_tier: &str,
        status: &str,
        raw_link: Option<&str>,
        rejection_reason: Option<&str>,
        declared_or_detected_gb: i64,
        fingerprint_sha256: Option<&str>,
        country_code: &str,
        country_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<DonatedOutbound, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO donated_outbounds (
                donor_user_id, tag, protocol, server_address, server_port, config_json,
                latency_ms, speed_mbps, assigned_tier, health_score, status, raw_link,
                rejection_reason, declared_or_detected_gb, bytes_served, monthly_reward_granted,
                fingerprint_sha256, country_code, country_name, last_checked_at
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 100, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(donor_user_id)
        .bind(tag)
        .bind(protocol)
        .bind(server_address)
        .bind(server_port)
        .bind(config_json)
        .bind(latency_ms)
        .bind(speed_mbps)
        .bind(assigned_tier)
        .bind(status)
        .bind(raw_link)
        .bind(rejection_reason)
        .bind(declared_or_detected_gb)
        .bind(fingerprint_sha256)
        .bind(country_code)
        .bind(country_name)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Record bytes served on active donated outbounds for this mode, and trigger 30-day Pro reward on first real usage if capacity > 200 GB
    pub async fn record_donated_outbound_usage(
        pool: &DbPool,
        mode: &str,
        delta_bytes: i64,
        bot_opt: Option<&teloxide::Bot>,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let active_donated = Self::get_active_donated_outbounds_for_mode(pool, mode).await.unwrap_or_default();
        for ob in active_donated {
            // 1. Update bytes_served and set first_used_at on first real traffic report
            let _ = sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE donated_outbounds
                SET bytes_served = bytes_served + ?,
                    first_used_at = COALESCE(first_used_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(delta_bytes)
            .bind(ob.id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await;

            // 2. Check reward trigger: community outbounds with capacity > 200 GB & not yet rewarded
            if ob.is_official == 0 && ob.declared_or_detected_gb > 200 && ob.monthly_reward_granted == 0 {
                if let Some(donor_uid) = ob.donor_user_id {
                    let is_banned = Self::is_user_reward_banned(pool, donor_uid).await.unwrap_or(false);
                    if !is_banned {
                        let _ = Self::upgrade_subscription_30_days(pool, donor_uid, "Sentra Pro").await;
                        let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE donated_outbounds SET monthly_reward_granted = 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?")
                            .bind(ob.id)
                            .execute(pool)
                            .await;

                        if let Some(bot) = bot_opt {
                            if let Ok(Some(donor_user)) = Self::get_user_by_id(pool, donor_uid).await {
                                if let Some(tid) = donor_user.telegram_id {
                                    use teloxide::prelude::*;
                                    use teloxide::types::{ChatId, ParseMode};
                                    let msg_text = format!(
                                        r#"ðŸŽ‰ <b>Ø§ÙˆØªØ¨Ø§Ù†Ø¯ Ø§Ù‡Ø¯Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø´Ù…Ø§ (Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø§ÛŒ Û²Û°Û° Ú¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª) Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú†Ø±Ø®Ù‡ Ø§Ø³ØªÙØ§Ø¯Ù‡ Ø´Ø¯!</b>

Ø¨Ù‡ Ù¾Ø§Ø³ Ø­Ù…Ø§ÛŒØª Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø§Ø² Ø´Ø¨Ú©Ù‡ Ø¶Ø¯Ø³Ø§Ù†Ø³ÙˆØ± SentraØŒ <b>Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú© Ø±Ø§ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ù† Ù…Ø§Ù‡Ø§Ù†Ù‡ (Û³Û° Ø±ÙˆØ²Ù‡ Sentra Pro)</b> Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ù…Ø§ ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ Ú¯Ø±Ø¯ÛŒØ¯. â¤ï¸

ðŸ“Š <b>Ù…Ø´Ø®ØµØ§Øª Ø§ÙˆØªØ¨Ø§Ù†Ø¯ ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡:</b>
â€¢ Ø¨Ø±Ú†Ø³Ø¨ Ù†ÙˆØ¯: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ Ù¾Ø±ÙˆØªÚ©Ù„: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ Ø¸Ø±ÙÛŒØª ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯Ø´Ø¯Ù‡: <b>{} Ú¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª</b>"#,
                                        ob.tag, ob.protocol, ob.declared_or_detected_gb
                                    );
                                    let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), msg_text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check if a receipt file or image hash was already submitted (Anti-Replay)
    pub async fn is_receipt_replay(
        pool: &DbPool,
        file_unique_id: &str,
        image_sha256: &str,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        let exists: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT id FROM donations WHERE file_unique_id = ? OR image_sha256 = ? LIMIT 1"
        )
        .bind(file_unique_id)
        .bind(image_sha256)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(exists.is_some())
    }

    /// Create a pending donation entry with unique file ID and image hash
    pub async fn create_donation(
        pool: &DbPool,
        user_id: i64,
        amount: i64,
        receipt_file_id: &str,
        file_unique_id: Option<&str>,
        image_sha256: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Donation, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, Donation>(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO donations (user_id, amount, receipt_file_id, file_unique_id, image_sha256, status, subscription_granted, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 0, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(user_id)
        .bind(amount)
        .bind(receipt_file_id)
        .bind(file_unique_id)
        .bind(image_sha256)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Get donation by id
    pub async fn get_donation_by_id(pool: &DbPool, donation_id: i64) -> Result<Option<Donation>, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, Donation>("SELECT * FROM donations WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1")
            .bind(donation_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Approve a donation, optionally granting 30 days of Sentra Pro
    pub async fn approve_donation(
        pool: &DbPool,
        donation_id: i64,
        grant_pro: bool,
    ) -> Result<Donation, CoreError> {
        let donation = Self::get_donation_by_id(pool, donation_id).await?
            .ok_or_else(|| CoreError::BadRequest("ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´ Ø­Ù…Ø§ÛŒØª Ù…Ø§Ù„ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.".to_string()))?;

        if grant_pro {
            let _ = Self::upgrade_subscription_30_days(pool, donation.user_id, "Sentra Pro").await?;
        }

        let updated = sqlx::query_as::<_, Donation>(
            r#"
            UPDATE donations
            SET status = 'approved',
                subscription_granted = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(if grant_pro { 1 } else { 0 })
        .bind(donation_id)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(updated)
    }

    /// Reject a donation
    pub async fn reject_donation(
        pool: &DbPool,
        donation_id: i64,
    ) -> Result<Donation, CoreError> {
        let updated = sqlx::query_as::<_, Donation>(
            r#"
            UPDATE donations
            SET status = 'rejected'
            WHERE id = ?
            RETURNING *
            "#
        )
        .bind(donation_id)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(updated)
    }

    /// Reward a donor user with 1 day of Sentra Pro tier gift
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn reward_donor_user(pool: &DbPool, donor_user_id: i64) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let sub_opt = Self::get_subscription_by_user_id(pool, donor_user_id).await.ok().flatten();
        if let Some(sub) = sub_opt {
            let new_tier = if sub.tier == "Sentra Lite" { "Sentra Pro" } else { &sub.tier };
            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE subscriptions 
                SET tier = ?,
                    expires_at = datetime(MAX(expires_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), '+1 day'),
                    updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(new_tier)
            .bind(sub.id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Retrieve active donated outbounds for a specific connection mode
    pub async fn get_active_donated_outbounds_for_mode(
        pool: &DbPool,
        mode: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE status = 'active' AND assigned_tier = ? ORDER BY health_score DESC, speed_mbps DESC LIMIT 10"
        )
        .bind(mode)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Retrieve all active donated outbounds for periodic 30-minute health checking
    pub async fn get_all_active_donated_outbounds(pool: &DbPool) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE status = 'active'"
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Update health check result for a donated outbound
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn update_donated_outbound_health(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
        latency_ms: i64,
        is_up: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        if is_up {
            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE donated_outbounds 
                SET latency_ms = ?, 
                    fail_count = 0, 
                    health_score = MIN(100, health_score + 5),
                    offline_since = NULL,
                    last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP 
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(latency_ms)
            .bind(id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        } else {
            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE donated_outbounds 
                SET fail_count = fail_count + 1,
                    health_score = MAX(0, health_score - 20),
                    offline_since = COALESCE(offline_since, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),
                    status = CASE WHEN fail_count >= 2 THEN 'inactive' ELSE status END,
                    last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP 
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Retrieve rewarded outbounds for monitoring 7-day stability & clawback eligibility
    pub async fn get_rewarded_donated_outbounds_for_clawback(pool: &DbPool) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds 
            WHERE monthly_reward_granted = 1 
              AND clawback_executed = 0 
              AND donor_user_id IS NOT NULL 
              AND first_used_at IS NOT NULL
              AND is_official = 0
            "#
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Execute clawback for an outbound that died within 7 days of reward
    pub async fn execute_outbound_clawback(
        pool: &DbPool,
        outbound_id: i64,
        donor_user_id: i64,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        // 1. Mark clawback executed on outbound
        sqlx::query("UPDATE donated_outbounds SET clawback_executed = 1, status = 'inactive' WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(outbound_id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;

        // 2. Ban user from future outbound donation rewards
        sqlx::query("UPDATE users SET outbound_reward_banned = 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(donor_user_id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;

        // 3. Revoke granted Pro subscription (downgrade to Sentra Lite)
        let sub = Self::get_subscription_by_user_id(pool, donor_user_id).await.ok().flatten();
        if let Some(s) = sub {
            if s.tier.contains("Pro") {
                sqlx::query(
                    r#"
                    UPDATE subscriptions
                    SET tier = 'Sentra Lite',
                        traffic_limit_bytes = 21474836480,
                        max_concurrent_devices = 3,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                    WHERE id = ?
                    "#
                )
                .bind(s.id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await?;
                return Ok(true);
            }
        }
        Ok(false)
    }

    /// Reset static IP quota (30 GB) for user
    pub async fn reset_user_static_ip_quota(pool: &DbPool, user_id: i64) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE subscriptions SET static_ip_used_mb = 0, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE user_id = ?"
        )
        .bind(user_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Retrieve all active Telegram IDs for broadcasting
    pub async fn get_all_active_telegram_ids(pool: &DbPool) -> Result<Vec<i64>, CoreError> {
        let ids: Vec<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT telegram_id FROM users WHERE status != 'banned' AND telegram_id IS NOT NULL")
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(ids)
    }

    /// Upsert an app release for a given platform ('windows' or 'android')
    pub async fn upsert_app_release(
        pool: &DbPool,
        platform: &str,
        version: &str,
        telegram_file_id: &str,
        file_name: &str,
        file_size_mb: f64,
        changelog: &str,
    ) -> Result<AppRelease, CoreError> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO app_releases (platform, version, telegram_file_id, file_name, file_size_mb, changelog, updated_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            ON CONFLICT(platform) DO UPDATE SET
                version = excluded.version,
                telegram_file_id = excluded.telegram_file_id,
                file_name = excluded.file_name,
                file_size_mb = excluded.file_size_mb,
                changelog = excluded.changelog,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            "#,
        )
        .bind(platform)
        .bind(version)
        .bind(telegram_file_id)
        .bind(file_name)
        .bind(file_size_mb)
        .bind(changelog)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        Self::get_app_release_by_platform(pool, platform)
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| CoreError::Database(sqlx::Error::RowNotFound))
    }

    /// Get an app release by platform ('windows' or 'android')
    pub async fn get_app_release_by_platform(
        pool: &DbPool,
        platform: &str,
    ) -> Result<Option<AppRelease>, CoreError> {
        let release = sqlx::query_as::<_, AppRelease>(
            r#"
            SELECT platform, version, telegram_file_id, file_name, file_size_mb, changelog, updated_at
            FROM app_releases
            WHERE platform = ?
            "#,
        )
        .bind(platform)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(release)
    }

    /// Get all app releases
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn get_all_app_releases(
        pool: &DbPool,
    ) -> Result<Vec<AppRelease>, CoreError> {
        let releases = sqlx::query_as::<_, AppRelease>(
            r#"
            SELECT platform, version, telegram_file_id, file_name, file_size_mb, changelog, updated_at
            FROM app_releases
            ORDER BY platform ASC
            "#,
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(releases)
    }

    /// Insert or update an official outbound node added by admin
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn insert_official_outbound(
        pool: &DbPool,
        label: &str,
        tag: &str,
        protocol: &str,
        server_address: &str,
        server_port: i64,
        config_json: &str,
        latency_ms: i64,
        speed_mbps: f64,
        category: &str,
        raw_link: Option<&str>,
        fingerprint_sha256: &str,
        status: &str,
        health_score: i64,
        country_code: &str,
        country_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<DonatedOutbound, CoreError> {
        let (allow_web, allow_download, allow_static_ip, alloc_mode, assigned_tier) = match category {
            "free_only" => (1, 1, 0, "free_only", "web_browsing"),
            "static_only" | "static_ip" => (0, 0, 1, "static_only", "static_ip"),
            "both" => (1, 1, 1, "both", "static_ip"),
            "auto" => (1, 1, 0, "auto", "web_browsing"),
            "download" => (0, 1, 0, "free_only", "download"),
            _ => (1, 0, 0, "free_only", "web_browsing"),
        };
        let speed_score = (60 - (latency_ms / 20)).clamp(10, 100);

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO donated_outbounds (
                donor_user_id, tag, protocol, server_address, server_port,
                config_json, latency_ms, speed_mbps, assigned_tier, health_score,
                status, fail_count, reward_granted, raw_link, rejection_reason,
                declared_or_detected_gb, bytes_served, first_used_at, monthly_reward_granted,
                fingerprint_sha256, offline_since, clawback_executed, is_official, label,
                country_code, country_name, last_checked_at, created_at, updated_at,
                allow_web, allow_download, allow_static_ip, gemini_ok, trading_safe,
                speed_score, jitter_ms, last_online_at, allocation_mode
            )
            VALUES (
                NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?,
                ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
                ?, 0, 0, ?, NULL,
                10000, 0, NULL, 0,
                ?, NULL, 0, 1, ?,
                ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                ?, ?, ?, 0, 0,
                ?, 0, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, ?
            )
            ON CONFLICT(fingerprint_sha256) DO UPDATE SET
                label = excluded.label,
                tag = excluded.tag,
                protocol = excluded.protocol,
                server_address = excluded.server_address,
                server_port = excluded.server_port,
                config_json = excluded.config_json,
                latency_ms = excluded.latency_ms,
                speed_mbps = excluded.speed_mbps,
                assigned_tier = excluded.assigned_tier,
                health_score = excluded.health_score,
                status = excluded.status,
                raw_link = excluded.raw_link,
                is_official = 1,
                country_code = excluded.country_code,
                country_name = excluded.country_name,
                allow_web = excluded.allow_web,
                allow_download = excluded.allow_download,
                allow_static_ip = excluded.allow_static_ip,
                speed_score = excluded.speed_score,
                allocation_mode = excluded.allocation_mode,
                last_online_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            "#,
        )
        .bind(tag)
        .bind(protocol)
        .bind(server_address)
        .bind(server_port)
        .bind(config_json)
        .bind(latency_ms)
        .bind(speed_mbps)
        .bind(assigned_tier)
        .bind(health_score)
        .bind(status)
        .bind(raw_link)
        .bind(fingerprint_sha256)
        .bind(label)
        .bind(country_code)
        .bind(country_name)
        .bind(allow_web)
        .bind(allow_download)
        .bind(allow_static_ip)
        .bind(speed_score)
        .bind(alloc_mode)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE fingerprint_sha256 = ?"
        )
        .bind(fingerprint_sha256)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Update allocation settings for an outbound
    pub async fn update_outbound_allocation(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
        allow_web: bool,
        allow_download: bool,
        allow_static_ip: bool,
        allocation_mode: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let assigned_tier = if allow_static_ip {
            "static_ip"
        } else if allow_download && !allow_web {
            "download"
        } else {
            "web_browsing"
        };

        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE donated_outbounds
            SET allow_web = ?,
                allow_download = ?,
                allow_static_ip = ?,
                allocation_mode = ?,
                assigned_tier = ?,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ?
            "#
        )
        .bind(if allow_web { 1 } else { 0 })
        .bind(if allow_download { 1 } else { 0 })
        .bind(if allow_static_ip { 1 } else { 0 })
        .bind(allocation_mode)
        .bind(assigned_tier)
        .bind(id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Update full quality metrics from quality engine benchmark
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn update_outbound_quality_metrics(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
        latency_ms: i64,
        jitter_ms: i64,
        speed_mbps: f64,
        speed_score: i64,
        gemini_ok: bool,
        trading_safe: bool,
        is_up: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let g_val = if gemini_ok { 1 } else { 0 };
        let t_val = if trading_safe { 1 } else { 0 };

        if is_up {
            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE donated_outbounds
                SET latency_ms = ?,
                    jitter_ms = ?,
                    speed_mbps = ?,
                    speed_score = ?,
                    gemini_ok = ?,
                    trading_safe = ?,
                    fail_count = 0,
                    health_score = MIN(100, health_score + 5),
                    offline_since = NULL,
                    last_online_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                    status = 'active',
                    allow_static_ip = CASE 
                        WHEN allocation_mode = 'auto' AND ? = 1 AND ? = 1 THEN 1
                        WHEN allocation_mode = 'auto' AND (? = 0 OR ? = 0) THEN 0
                        ELSE allow_static_ip
                    END,
                    assigned_tier = CASE
                        WHEN allocation_mode = 'auto' AND ? = 1 AND ? = 1 THEN 'static_ip'
                        ELSE assigned_tier
                    END
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(latency_ms)
            .bind(jitter_ms)
            .bind(speed_mbps)
            .bind(speed_score)
            .bind(g_val)
            .bind(t_val)
            .bind(g_val)
            .bind(t_val)
            .bind(g_val)
            .bind(t_val)
            .bind(g_val)
            .bind(t_val)
            .bind(id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        } else {
            sqlx::query(
                r#"
                UPDATE donated_outbounds
                SET fail_count = fail_count + 1,
                    health_score = MAX(0, health_score - 20),
                    offline_since = COALESCE(offline_since, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP),
                    status = CASE WHEN fail_count >= 2 THEN 'inactive' ELSE status END,
                    last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                WHERE id = ?
                "#
            )
            .bind(id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Automatically clean up dead outbounds older than 48 hours without successful response
    pub async fn cleanup_dead_outbounds(pool: &DbPool) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let dead_nodes = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds
            WHERE (last_online_at IS NOT NULL AND last_online_at < datetime('now', '-48 hours'))
               OR (last_online_at IS NULL AND created_at < datetime('now', '-48 hours') AND status = 'inactive')
            "#
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;

        if !dead_nodes.is_empty() {
            for node in &dead_nodes {
                let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM donated_outbounds WHERE id = ?")
                    .bind(node.id)
                    .execute(pool)
                    .await?;
            }
        }

        Ok(dead_nodes)
    }

    /// Retrieve any donated or official outbound by ID
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn get_donated_outbound_by_id(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
    ) -> Result<Option<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE id = ?"
        )
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Retrieve official outbounds registered for a specific category ('web_browsing', 'download', 'static_ip')
    pub async fn get_official_outbounds_by_category(
        pool: &DbPool,
        category: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let filter_col = match category {
            "static_ip" | "static_only" => "allow_static_ip = 1",
            "download" => "allow_download = 1",
            _ => "allow_web = 1",
        };

        let query = format!(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds 
            WHERE is_official = 1 AND ({} OR assigned_tier = ?) 
            ORDER BY speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC, id DESC
            "#,
            filter_col
        );

        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(&query)
            .bind(category)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Retrieve all official outbounds
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn get_all_official_outbounds(
        pool: &DbPool,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE is_official = 1 ORDER BY speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC, id DESC"
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Retrieve a single official outbound by ID
    pub async fn get_official_outbound_by_id(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
    ) -> Result<Option<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(
            "SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds WHERE id = ? AND is_official = 1"
        )
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Toggle official outbound status between active and inactive
    pub async fn toggle_official_outbound_status(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
    ) -> Result<String, CoreError> {
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE donated_outbounds 
            SET status = CASE WHEN status = 'active' THEN 'inactive' ELSE 'active' END,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ? AND is_official = 1
            "#
        )
        .bind(id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;

        let status: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT status FROM donated_outbounds WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(id)
            .fetch_one(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(status)
    }

    /// Delete an official outbound
    pub async fn delete_official_outbound(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        let res = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM donated_outbounds WHERE id = ? AND is_official = 1")
            .bind(id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(res.rows_affected() > 0)
    }

    /// Update official outbound benchmark results
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn update_official_outbound_benchmark(
        pool: &DbPool,
        id: i64,
        latency_ms: i64,
        speed_mbps: f64,
        is_online: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let status = if is_online { "active" } else { "inactive" };
        let health_score = if is_online { 100 } else { 0 };
        sqlx::query(
            r#"
            UPDATE donated_outbounds
            SET latency_ms = ?,
                speed_mbps = ?,
                status = ?,
                health_score = ?,
                last_checked_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ? AND is_official = 1
            "#
        )
        .bind(latency_ms)
        .bind(speed_mbps)
        .bind(status)
        .bind(health_score)
        .bind(id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Retrieve active official outbounds for static_ip mode (and community nodes qualified for static_ip)
    pub async fn get_active_official_outbounds_for_mode(
        pool: &DbPool,
        mode: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let (filter_col, official_cond) = if mode == "static_ip" {
            // Include official static outbounds and all nodes qualified for static_ip (e.g. gemini/trading passed)
            ("(allow_static_ip = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'static_ip')", "")
        } else if mode == "download" {
            ("(allow_download = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'download')", "AND is_official = 1")
        } else {
            ("(allow_web = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'web_browsing')", "AND is_official = 1")
        };

        let query = format!(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds 
            WHERE status = 'active' AND {} {}
            ORDER BY is_official DESC, speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC, health_score DESC
            "#,
            filter_col, official_cond
        );

        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(&query)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Retrieve active outbounds for client modes (web_browsing and download), prioritizing official outbounds
    pub async fn get_active_outbounds_for_mode_prioritized(
        pool: &DbPool,
        mode: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let filter_col = match mode {
            "static_ip" => "(allow_static_ip = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'static_ip')",
            "download" => "(allow_download = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'download')",
            _ => "(allow_web = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'web_browsing')",
        };

        let query = format!(
            r#"
            SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds 
            WHERE status = 'active' AND {}
            ORDER BY is_official DESC, speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC, health_score DESC
            LIMIT 15
            "#,
            filter_col
        );

        let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(&query)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(rows)
    }

    /// Retrieve active outbounds for a mode and optional location code (with auto fallback)
    pub async fn get_active_outbounds_for_mode_and_location(
        pool: &DbPool,
        mode: &str,
        location_code: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DonatedOutbound>, CoreError> {
        let code = location_code.unwrap_or("auto").trim();
        let filter_col = match mode {
            "static_ip" => "(allow_static_ip = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'static_ip')",
            "download" => "(allow_download = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'download')",
            _ => "(allow_web = 1 OR assigned_tier = 'web_browsing')",
        };

        if !code.is_empty() && !code.eq_ignore_ascii_case("auto") {
            let query = format!(
                r#"
                SELECT * FROM donated_outbounds 
                WHERE status = 'active' AND {} AND UPPER(country_code) = UPPER(?)
                ORDER BY is_official DESC, speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC, health_score DESC
                LIMIT 15
                "#,
                filter_col
            );

            let loc_rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, DonatedOutbound>(&query)
                .bind(code)
                .fetch_all(pool)
                .await?;

            if !loc_rows.is_empty() {
                return Ok(loc_rows);
            }
        }

        // Default or fallback to fastest active nodes
        Self::get_active_outbounds_for_mode_prioritized(pool, mode).await
    }

    /// Retrieve active locations list for client location switcher
    pub async fn get_active_locations_for_free_modes(
        pool: &DbPool,
    ) -> Result<Vec<crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo>, CoreError> {
        use sqlx::Row;

        let rows = sqlx::query(
            r#"
            SELECT 
                country_code,
                country_name,
                MIN(latency_ms) as min_ping,
                COUNT(*) as active_count
            FROM donated_outbounds
            WHERE status = 'active' 
              AND (allow_web = 1 OR allow_download = 1 OR assigned_tier IN ('web_browsing', 'download'))
              AND country_code != 'XX'
            GROUP BY country_code, country_name
            HAVING active_count > 0
            ORDER BY min_ping ASC
            "#
        )
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .unwrap_or_default();

        let mut locations = Vec::new();
        let mut total_active_nodes = 0i64;
        let mut best_auto_ping = 45i64;

        for (idx, r) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
            let code: String = r.try_get("country_code").unwrap_or_else(|_| "XX".to_string());
            let name_raw: String = r.try_get("country_name").unwrap_or_else(|_| "Unknown".to_string());
            let ping: i64 = r.try_get("min_ping").unwrap_or(99);
            let count: i64 = r.try_get("active_count").unwrap_or(1);

            total_active_nodes += count;
            if idx == 0 {
                best_auto_ping = ping;
            }

            let name = if name_raw.is_empty() || name_raw == "Unknown" {
                crate::outbound_analyzer::OutboundAnalyzer::country_code_to_name(&code)
            } else {
                name_raw
            };
            let flag = crate::outbound_analyzer::OutboundAnalyzer::country_code_to_flag(&code);

            locations.push(crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo {
                code: code.clone(),
                name,
                country_code: code,
                flag,
                ping_ms: ping,
                active_nodes: count,
                is_auto: false,
            });
        }

        // Add Auto option as the very first choice
        let mut final_locations = Vec::new();
        final_locations.push(crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo {
            code: "auto".to_string(),
            name: "âš¡ï¸ Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø³Ø±ÛŒØ¹â€ŒØªØ±ÛŒÙ† Ù„ÙˆÚ©ÛŒØ´Ù† (Auto)".to_string(),
            country_code: "AUTO".to_string(),
            flag: "âš¡ï¸".to_string(),
            ping_ms: best_auto_ping,
            active_nodes: total_active_nodes.max(1),
            is_auto: true,
        });
        final_locations.extend(locations);

        // If no locations configured yet in local dev environment, provide default fast locations
        if final_locations.len() <= 1 {
            final_locations.push(crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo {
                code: "DE".to_string(),
                name: "Ø¢Ù„Ù…Ø§Ù† ðŸ‡©ðŸ‡ª".to_string(),
                country_code: "DE".to_string(),
                flag: "ðŸ‡©ðŸ‡ª".to_string(),
                ping_ms: 48,
                active_nodes: 3,
                is_auto: false,
            });
            final_locations.push(crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo {
                code: "NL".to_string(),
                name: "Ù‡Ù„Ù†Ø¯ ðŸ‡³ðŸ‡±".to_string(),
                country_code: "NL".to_string(),
                flag: "ðŸ‡³ðŸ‡±".to_string(),
                ping_ms: 62,
                active_nodes: 2,
                is_auto: false,
            });
            final_locations.push(crate::models::ActiveLocationInfo {
                code: "FI".to_string(),
                name: "ÙÙ†Ù„Ø§Ù†Ø¯ ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡®".to_string(),
                country_code: "FI".to_string(),
                flag: "ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡®".to_string(),
                ping_ms: 70,
                active_nodes: 1,
                is_auto: false,
            });
        }

        Ok(final_locations)
    }

    /// Save or update subscription source URL in database
    pub async fn save_subscription_source(
        pool: &DbPool,
        category: &str,
        url: &str,
        label: Option<&str>,
        node_count: i64,
        healthy_count: i64,
    ) -> Result<i64, CoreError> {
        use sqlx::Row;
        let row = sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO subscription_sources (category, url, label, node_count, healthy_count, last_synced_at, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
            ON CONFLICT(url) DO UPDATE SET
                category = excluded.category,
                label = excluded.label,
                node_count = excluded.node_count,
                healthy_count = excluded.healthy_count,
                last_synced_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            RETURNING id
            "#
        )
        .bind(category)
        .bind(url)
        .bind(label)
        .bind(node_count)
        .bind(healthy_count)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        let id: i64 = row.try_get("id").unwrap_or(1);
        Ok(id)
    }

    /// Retrieve subscription source by ID
    pub async fn get_subscription_source_by_id(

            "SELECT * FROM subscription_sources WHERE id = ?"
        )
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Save or replace active Cloudflare WARP account
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub async fn save_warp_account(
        pool: &DbPool,
        private_key: &str,
        public_key: &str,
        address_v4: &str,
        address_v6: &str,
        reserved: &str,
        account_id: Option<&str>,
        token: Option<&str>,
        license_key: Option<&str>,
        account_type: &str,
    ) -> Result<i64, CoreError> {
        // Deactivate previous WARP accounts to keep a single active master
        let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE warp_accounts SET is_active = 0").execute(pool).await;

        let row = sqlx::query(
            r#"
            INSERT INTO warp_accounts (
                private_key, public_key, address_v4, address_v6, reserved,
                account_id, token, license_key, account_type, is_active
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1)
            RETURNING id
            "#
        )
        .bind(private_key)
        .bind(public_key)
        .bind(address_v4)
        .bind(address_v6)
        .bind(reserved)
        .bind(account_id)
        .bind(token)
        .bind(license_key)
        .bind(account_type)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await?;

        let id: i64 = row.try_get("id").unwrap_or(1);
        Ok(id)
    }

    /// Get current active Cloudflare WARP account
    pub async fn get_active_warp_account(
        pool: &DbPool,
    ) -> Result<Option<crate::models::WarpAccount>, CoreError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as::<_, crate::models::WarpAccount>(
            "SELECT * FROM warp_accounts WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        )
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(row)
    }

    /// Update user session WARP preference
    pub async fn update_device_use_warp(
        pool: &DbPool,
        subscription_id: i64,
        device_identifier: &str,
        use_warp: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
        let warp_val = if use_warp { 1 } else { 0 };
        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE device_sessions SET use_warp = ? WHERE subscription_id = ? AND device_identifier = ?"
        )
        .bind(warp_val)
        .bind(subscription_id)
        .bind(device_identifier)
        .execute(pool)
        .await;
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "action", rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum HeartbeatVerdict {
    Continue {
        remaining_bytes: i64,
        web_browsing_used_mb: i64,
        download_used_mb: i64,
        static_ip_used_mb: i64,
        static_ip_remaining_mb: i64,
    },
    Disconnect {
        code: String,
        message: String,
    },
}

pub struct AdminRealtimeStats {

    pub active_sessions: i64,
    pub traffic_24h_bytes: i64,
}


=== FILE: sentra-core\src\anomaly_detector.rs ===
use crate::db::{DbPool, Repository};
use crate::error::CoreError;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use teloxide::prelude::*;
use teloxide::types::{ChatId, InlineKeyboardButton, InlineKeyboardMarkup, ParseMode};
use tokio::sync::RwLock;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct TrafficEntry {
    timestamp: Instant,
    bytes: i64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AnomalyDetector {
    traffic_window: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<i64, Vec<TrafficEntry>>>>,
}

impl AnomalyDetector {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            traffic_window: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Records traffic delta and evaluates sliding window limits:
    /// - 15 minutes threshold: 5 GB (5,368,709,120 bytes)
    /// - 60 minutes threshold: 20 GB (21,474,836,480 bytes)
    pub async fn record_and_check(
        &self,
        pool: &DbPool,
        user_id: i64,
        bytes_delta: i64,
        bot_opt: Option<&Bot>,
        admin_chat_id: i64,
    ) -> Result<bool, CoreError> {
        if bytes_delta <= 0 {
            return Ok(false);
        }

        let now = Instant::now();
        let one_hour = Duration::from_secs(3600);
        let fifteen_mins = Duration::from_secs(900);

        let limit_15m: i64 = 5 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 GB
        let limit_60m: i64 = 20 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024; // 20 GB

        let mut map = self.traffic_window.write().await;
        let entries = map.entry(user_id).or_insert_with(Vec::new);

        entries.push(TrafficEntry {
            timestamp: now,
            bytes: bytes_delta,
        });

        // Retain only entries within the last 1 hour
        entries.retain(|e| now.duration_since(e.timestamp) <= one_hour);

        let mut sum_15m: i64 = 0;
        let mut sum_60m: i64 = 0;

        for e in entries.iter() {
            let age = now.duration_since(e.timestamp);
            sum_60m += e.bytes;
            if age <= fifteen_mins {
                sum_15m += e.bytes;
            }
        }

        let is_anomaly_15m = sum_15m > limit_15m;
        let is_anomaly_60m = sum_60m > limit_60m;

        if is_anomaly_15m || is_anomaly_60m {
            let reason = if is_anomaly_15m {
                format!(
                    "Ù…ØµØ±Ù Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Ûµ Ú¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª Ø¯Ø± Û±Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡ ({:.2} GB Ø¯Ø± Û±Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡)",
                    sum_15m as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0)
                )
            } else {
                format!(
                    "Ù…ØµØ±Ù Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Û²Û° Ú¯ÛŒÚ¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª Ø¯Ø± Û± Ø³Ø§Ø¹Øª ({:.2} GB Ø¯Ø± Û¶Û° Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡)",
                    sum_60m as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0)
                )
            };

            // 1. Suspend user immediately in Database
            Repository::suspend_user_account(pool, user_id, &reason).await?;

            // 2. Dispatch urgent Telegram alert to ADMIN_CHAT_ID
            if let Some(bot) = bot_opt {
                if admin_chat_id != 0 {
                    let user_res = Repository::get_user_by_id(pool, user_id).await?;
                    let (uname, fname, t_id) = if let Some(u) = user_res {
                        (
                            u.username.unwrap_or_else(|| "Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯".to_string()),
                            u.first_name,
                            u.telegram_id.unwrap_or(0),
                        )
                    } else {
                        ("Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø´Ø®Øµ".to_string(), "Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±".to_string(), 0)
                    };

                        r#"ðŸš¨ <b>Ù‡Ø´Ø¯Ø§Ø± Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ: ØªØ´Ø®ÛŒØµ Ù…ØµØ±Ù ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ© Ù…Ø´Ú©ÙˆÚ© (Anomaly Detected)</b>

â€¢ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ…: <code>{}</code>
â€¢ Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ: @{}
â€¢ Ù†Ø§Ù… Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±: <b>{}</b>
â€¢ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ø¹Ø¯Ø¯ÛŒ ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù…: <code>{}</code>

â€¢ ØªØ®Ù„Ù: <b>{}</b>
â€¢ ØªØ­Ù„ÛŒÙ„ ØªÙ‡Ø¯ÛŒØ¯: <b>Ø§Ø­ØªÙ…Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ø´ØªØ±Ø§Ú©â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯ Ø¨Ø§ Ø¯Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ù†ÙØ± ÛŒØ§ Ø­Ù…Ù„Ù‡ ÙÙ„ÙˆØ¯ ØªØ±Ø§ÙÛŒÚ©ÛŒ</b>

                        user_id, uname, fname, t_id, reason

                        InlineKeyboardButton::callback(
                            "âœ… Ø±ÙØ¹ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ÛŒØª Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±",
                            format!("admin_unsuspend_{}", user_id),

                        .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
                        .reply_markup(keyboard)
                        .await;
                }
            }

            return Ok(true);
        }

        Ok(false)
    }

    /// Reset sliding window after admin unsuspends user
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub async fn reset_user(&self, user_id: i64) {
        let mut map = self.traffic_window.write().await;
        map.remove(&user_id);
    }
}


=== FILE: sentra-core\src\bridge_sync.rs ===
use crate::db::{DbPool, Repository};
use crate::error::CoreError;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::env;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use tracing::{info, warn};

/// Synchronizes server-side sing-box bridge configuration file (/etc/sing-box/bridge-config.json)
/// and gracefully triggers sing-box reload without dropping active connections.
pub async fn sync_server_bridge_config(pool: &DbPool) -> Result<(), CoreError> {
    info!("[BridgeSync] Synchronizing server sing-box bridge configuration...");

    // 1. Fetch active outbounds for the 3 modes
    // Static IP: strictly official only
    let static_outbounds = Repository::get_active_official_outbounds_for_mode(pool, "static_ip")
        .await
        .unwrap_or_default();

    // Web Browsing: prioritized official then donated
    let web_outbounds = Repository::get_active_outbounds_for_mode_prioritized(pool, "web_browsing")
        .await
        .unwrap_or_default();

    // Download: prioritized official then donated
    let dl_outbounds = Repository::get_active_outbounds_for_mode_prioritized(pool, "download")
        .await
        .unwrap_or_default();

    let mut singbox_outbounds: Vec<Value> = Vec::new();
    let mut web_outbound_tags: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut dl_outbound_tags: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut static_outbound_tags: Vec<String> = Vec::new();

    // Default direct outbound fallback
    singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
        "type": "direct",
        "tag": "direct-out"
    }));

    // Register all active web outbounds with country tags (sorted by speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC)
    for dob in &web_outbounds {
        if let Ok(mut parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&dob.config_json) {
            let tag = format!("out-web-{}-{}", dob.country_code.to_lowercase(), dob.id);
            if let Some(obj) = parsed.as_object_mut() {
                obj.insert("tag".to_string(), json!(tag));
            }
            singbox_outbounds.push(parsed);
            web_outbound_tags.push(tag);
        }
    }

    // Register all active download outbounds with country tags (sorted by speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC)
    for dob in &dl_outbounds {
        if let Ok(mut parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&dob.config_json) {
            let tag = format!("out-dl-{}-{}", dob.country_code.to_lowercase(), dob.id);
            if let Some(obj) = parsed.as_object_mut() {
                obj.insert("tag".to_string(), json!(tag));
            }
            singbox_outbounds.push(parsed);
            dl_outbound_tags.push(tag);
        }
    }

    // Register static_ip outbounds (sorted by speed_score DESC, latency_ms ASC)
    for dob in &static_outbounds {
        if let Ok(mut parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&dob.config_json) {
            let tag = format!("out-static-{}-{}", dob.country_code.to_lowercase(), dob.id);
            if let Some(obj) = parsed.as_object_mut() {
                obj.insert("tag".to_string(), json!(tag));
            }
            singbox_outbounds.push(parsed);
            static_outbound_tags.push(tag);
        }
    }

    let static_target_tag = if !static_outbound_tags.is_empty() {
        if static_outbound_tags.len() > 1 {
            singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
                "type": "urltest",
                "tag": "out-static-auto",
                "outbounds": static_outbound_tags,
                "url": "https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ping",
                "interval": "5m",
                "tolerance": 30
            }));
            "out-static-auto".to_string()
        } else {
            static_outbound_tags[0].clone()
        }
    } else {
        "direct-out".to_string()
    };

    // Create urltest Auto groups for web and download
    let web_target_tag = if !web_outbound_tags.is_empty() {
        singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "urltest",
            "tag": "out-web-auto",
            "outbounds": web_outbound_tags,
            "url": "http://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_204",
            "interval": "3m",
            "tolerance": 50
        }));
        "out-web-auto".to_string()
    } else {
        "direct-out".to_string()
    };

    let dl_target_tag = if !dl_outbound_tags.is_empty() {
        singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "urltest",
            "tag": "out-dl-auto",
            "outbounds": dl_outbound_tags,
            "url": "http://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_204",
            "interval": "3m",
            "tolerance": 50
        }));
        "out-dl-auto".to_string()
    } else {
        "direct-out".to_string()
    };

    // 2. Fetch active Cloudflare WARP account for dual-chain routing
    let warp_opt = Repository::get_active_warp_account(pool).await.unwrap_or(None);

    let mut inbounds = vec![
        json!({
            "type": "tun",
            "tag": "tun-web",
            "interface_name": "tun-web",
            "inet4_address": "10.10.0.1/30",
            "auto_route": false,
            "strict_route": false,
            "stack": "system"
        }),
        json!({
            "type": "tun",
            "tag": "tun-dl",
            "interface_name": "tun-dl",
            "inet4_address": "10.10.1.1/30",
            "auto_route": false,
            "strict_route": false,
            "stack": "system"
        }),
        json!({
            "type": "tun",
            "tag": "tun-static",
            "interface_name": "tun-static",
            "inet4_address": "10.10.2.1/30",
            "auto_route": false,
            "strict_route": false,
            "stack": "system"
        }),
    ];

    let mut route_rules = vec![
        json!({
            "inbound": ["tun-web"],
            "outbound": web_target_tag
        }),
        json!({
            "inbound": ["tun-dl"],
            "outbound": dl_target_tag
        }),
        json!({
            "inbound": ["tun-static"],
            "outbound": static_target_tag
        }),
    ];

    if let Some(warp) = warp_opt {
        let reserved_val: Value = serde_json::from_str(&warp.reserved).unwrap_or_else(|_| json!([0, 0, 0]));

        // WARP chained outbound for Web Browsing (detour -> out-web-auto)
        singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "wireguard",
            "tag": "out-web-warp",
            "server": "162.159.192.1",
            "server_port": 2408,
            "local_address": [
                format!("{}/32", warp.address_v4),
                format!("{}/128", warp.address_v6)
            ],
            "private_key": warp.private_key,
            "peer_public_key": "bmXOC+F1FxEMF9dyiK2H5/1SUtzH0JuVo51h2wPfgyo=",
            "reserved": reserved_val,
            "mtu": 1280,
            "detour": web_target_tag
        }));

        // WARP chained outbound for Download (detour -> out-dl-auto)
        singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "wireguard",
            "tag": "out-dl-warp",
            "server": "162.159.192.1",
            "server_port": 2408,
            "local_address": [
                format!("{}/32", warp.address_v4),
                format!("{}/128", warp.address_v6)
            ],
            "private_key": warp.private_key,
            "peer_public_key": "bmXOC+F1FxEMF9dyiK2H5/1SUtzH0JuVo51h2wPfgyo=",
            "reserved": reserved_val,
            "mtu": 1280,
            "detour": dl_target_tag
        }));

        // Add dedicated TUN inbounds for WARP traffic
        inbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "tun",
            "tag": "tun-web-warp",
            "interface_name": "tun-web-warp",
            "inet4_address": "10.10.10.1/30",
            "auto_route": false,
            "strict_route": false,
            "stack": "system"
        }));
        inbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "tun",
            "tag": "tun-dl-warp",
            "interface_name": "tun-dl-warp",
            "inet4_address": "10.10.11.1/30",
            "auto_route": false,
            "strict_route": false,
            "stack": "system"
        }));

        // Direct WARP inbounds through chained wireguard outbounds
        route_rules.insert(0, json!({
            "inbound": ["tun-web-warp"],
            "outbound": "out-web-warp"
        }));
        route_rules.insert(1, json!({
            "inbound": ["tun-dl-warp"],
            "outbound": "out-dl-warp"
        }));
    }

    let config = json!({
        "log": {
            "level": "warn",
            "timestamp": true
        },
        "inbounds": inbounds,
        "outbounds": singbox_outbounds,
        "route": {
            "rules": route_rules,
            "final": "direct-out"
        }
    });

    let bridge_path_str = env::var("SINGBOX_BRIDGE_PATH")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            if Path::new("/etc/sing-box/config.json").exists() || Path::new("/etc/sing-box").exists() {
                "/etc/sing-box/config.json".to_string()
            } else {
                "/etc/sing-box/bridge-config.json".to_string()
            }
        });
    let bridge_path = Path::new(&bridge_path_str);

    if let Some(parent) = bridge_path.parent() {
        let _ = fs::create_dir_all(parent);
    }

    let formatted_json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&config)
        .map_err(|e| CoreError::Internal(format!("Failed to serialize bridge config: {}", e)))?;

    match fs::write(bridge_path, &formatted_json) {
        Ok(_) => {
            info!(
                "[BridgeSync] Successfully written bridge config to {:?}. Reloading sing-box...",
                bridge_path
            );
            // Also write mirror copy to /etc/sing-box/bridge-config.json if target was config.json
            if bridge_path_str.ends_with("config.json") {
                let _ = fs::write("/etc/sing-box/bridge-config.json", &formatted_json);
            }
            reload_singbox_service();
        }
        Err(e) => {
            warn!(
                "[BridgeSync] Could not write to {:?} ({}). Saving to local fallback .sentra/bridge-config.json",
                bridge_path, e
            );
            let local_fallback = Path::new(".sentra/bridge-config.json");
            if let Some(p) = local_fallback.parent() {
                let _ = fs::create_dir_all(p);
            }
            if let Ok(c) = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&config) {
                let _ = fs::write(local_fallback, c);
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

fn reload_singbox_service() {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::process::Command;
        info!("[BridgeSync] Executing systemctl reload sing-box...");
        let status = Command::new("systemctl")
            .args(["reload", "sing-box"])
            .status();

        match status {
            Ok(s) if s.success() => {
                info!("[BridgeSync] sing-box reloaded successfully via systemctl.");
            }
            _ => {
                warn!("[BridgeSync] systemctl reload failed. Attempting SIGHUP killall -HUP sing-box...");
                let _ = Command::new("killall")
                    .args(["-HUP", "sing-box"])
                    .status();
            }
        }
    }

    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    {
        info!("[BridgeSync] Non-unix platform detected. Reload signal simulated for sing-box bridge.");
    }
}
