use crate::models::{DonatedOutbound, Outbound, Subscription};
use chrono::Utc;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

/// Compiles a fully compliant Sing-box v1.10+ JSON template tailored to the user's subscription tier
/// and allowed outbounds, strictly following docs/PROTOCOL_SPEC.md.
pub fn compile_singbox_config(
    subscription: &Subscription,
    outbounds: &[Outbound],
    preferred_mode: Option<&str>,
) -> Value {
    compile_singbox_config_with_donated(subscription, outbounds, &[], preferred_mode)
}

/// Compiles Sing-box v1.10+ JSON including active community donated outbounds
pub fn compile_singbox_config_with_donated(
    subscription: &Subscription,
    outbounds: &[Outbound],
    donated_outbounds: &[DonatedOutbound],
    preferred_mode: Option<&str>,
) -> Value {
    let mut outbound_tags: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut singbox_outbounds: Vec<Value> = Vec::new();

    for ob in outbounds {
        outbound_tags.push(ob.tag.clone());

        let node_config = match ob.protocol.as_str() {
            "socks" => json!({
                "type": "socks",
                "tag": ob.tag,
                "server": ob.server_address,
                "server_port": ob.server_port,
                "version": "5"
            }),
            "http" => json!({
                "type": "http",
                "tag": ob.tag,
                "server": ob.server_address,
                "server_port": ob.server_port
            }),
            "vless" => {
                let mut vless_obj = json!({
                    "type": "vless",
                    "tag": ob.tag,
                    "server": ob.server_address,
                    "server_port": ob.server_port,
                    "uuid": ob.uuid.as_deref().unwrap_or("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000"),
                    "flow": "",
                    "packet_encoding": "xudp"
                });
                if ob.tls_enabled == 1 {
                    vless_obj["tls"] = json!({
                        "enabled": true,
                        "server_name": ob.tls_sni.as_deref().unwrap_or("learn.microsoft.com"),
                        "utls": {
                            "enabled": true,
                            "fingerprint": "chrome"
                        },
                        "reality": {
                            "enabled": true,
                            "public_key": ob.reality_public_key.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
                            "short_id": ob.reality_short_id.as_deref().unwrap_or("")
                        }
                    });
                }
                vless_obj
            }
            "trojan" => json!({
                "type": "trojan",
                "tag": ob.tag,
                "server": ob.server_address,
                "server_port": ob.server_port,
                "password": ob.password.as_deref().unwrap_or("sentra_pass")
            }),
            "hysteria2" => json!({
                "type": "hysteria2",
                "tag": ob.tag,
                "server": ob.server_address,
                "server_port": ob.server_port,
                "password": ob.password.as_deref().unwrap_or("sentra_pass")
            }),
            "direct" => json!({
                "type": "direct",
                "tag": ob.tag
            }),
            _ => json!({
                "type": "socks",
                "tag": ob.tag,
                "server": ob.server_address,
                "server_port": ob.server_port,
                "version": "5"
            }),
        };

        singbox_outbounds.push(node_config);
    }

    // Incorporate active official & donated outbounds (parsed JSON config)
    for dob in donated_outbounds {
        if let Ok(mut val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&dob.config_json) {
            let tag = if dob.is_official == 1 {
                if let Some(ref l) = dob.label {
                    format!("ðŸ‘‘ {}", l)
                } else {
                    format!("ðŸ‘‘ {}", dob.tag)
                }
            } else {
                format!("ðŸ¤ {}", dob.tag)
            };
            val["tag"] = json!(tag);
            outbound_tags.push(tag);
            singbox_outbounds.push(val);
        }
    }

    // Fallback if no specific outbounds were matched
    if outbound_tags.is_empty() {
        outbound_tags.push("Sentra Sandbox Loopback".to_string());
        singbox_outbounds.push(json!({
            "type": "socks",
            "tag": "Sentra Sandbox Loopback",
            "server": "127.0.0.1",
            "server_port": 10801,
            "version": "5"
        }));
    }

    // Build the master Outbound list
    let mut all_outbounds = Vec::new();

    // 1. Selector Group (Sentra Select)
    let mut selector_list = vec!["Sentra Auto".to_string()];
    selector_list.extend(outbound_tags.clone());
    selector_list.push("direct".to_string());

    all_outbounds.push(json!({
        "type": "selector",
        "tag": "Sentra Select",
        "outbounds": selector_list,
        "default": "Sentra Auto"
    }));

    // 2. Latency auto-switching Group (Sentra Auto) - 15s interval
    all_outbounds.push(json!({
        "type": "urltest",
        "tag": "Sentra Auto",
        "outbounds": outbound_tags,
        "url": "https://cp.cloudflare.com/generate_204",
        "interval": "15s",
        "tolerance": 50
    }));

    // 3. Concrete mode outbounds
    all_outbounds.extend(singbox_outbounds);

    // 4. Base system outbounds
    all_outbounds.push(json!({ "type": "direct", "tag": "direct" }));
    all_outbounds.push(json!({ "type": "block", "tag": "block" }));
    all_outbounds.push(json!({ "type": "dns", "tag": "dns-out" }));

    // Iranian domain bypass list for split tunneling
    let ir_domain_suffixes = vec![
        ".ir",
        ".gov.ir",
        ".co.ir",
        "shaparak.ir",
        "divar.ir",
        "digikala.com",
        "snapp.ir",
        "tamin.ir",
        "bmi.ir",
        "bki.ir",
        "mellatbank.ir",
        "tejaratbank.ir",
        "banksepah.ir"
    ];

    // Assembly conforming to Sing-box v1.10+
    json!({
        "_sentra_meta": {
            "tier": subscription.tier,
            "status": subscription.status,
            "mode": preferred_mode.unwrap_or("web_browsing"),
            "traffic_limit_bytes": subscription.traffic_limit_bytes,
            "used_bytes": subscription.upload_bytes + subscription.download_bytes,
            "web_browsing_used_mb": subscription.web_browsing_used_mb,
            "download_used_mb": subscription.download_used_mb,
            "speed_limit_bps": subscription.speed_limit_bps,
            "expires_at": subscription.expires_at.to_rfc3339()
        },
        "log": {
            "level": "info",
            "timestamp": true
        },
        "dns": {
            "servers": [
                {
                    "tag": "dns-fakeip",
                    "address": "fakeip"
                },
                {
                    "tag": "dns-doh",
                    "address": "https://1.1.1.1/dns-query",

/// Generates a valid OpenVPN (.ovpn) configuration file representation
pub fn generate_ovpn_profile(subscription: &Subscription, mode: Option<&str>) -> String {
    generate_ovpn_profile_ext(subscription, mode, "openvpn_udp", "127.0.0.1")
}

/// Generates a multi-protocol OpenVPN configuration file (.ovpn) tailored to mode and protocol
pub fn generate_ovpn_profile_ext(
    subscription: &Subscription,
    mode: Option<&str>,
    protocol: &str,
    server_host: &str,
) -> String {
    let mode_str = mode.unwrap_or("web_browsing");
    let mode_label = match mode_str {
        "download" => "Download (High Speed / Hysteria2-Tunnel)",
        "static_ip" => "Static IP (Clean US Residential / Trade & AI Ready)",
        "gaming" => "Gaming (Low Ping / UDP Optimized)",
        _ => "Web Browsing (Everyday / WS+Fragment)",
    };

    let is_tcp = protocol.eq_ignore_ascii_case("openvpn_tcp") || protocol.eq_ignore_ascii_case("tcp");
    let is_stealth = protocol.eq_ignore_ascii_case("stealth");

    let (proto_str, target_host, target_port) = if is_stealth {
        ("tcp-client", "127.0.0.1", 11940)
    } else if is_tcp {
        let p = match mode_str {
            "download" => 2053,
            "static_ip" => 2083,
            _ => 443,
        };
        ("tcp-client", server_host, p)
    } else {
        let p = match mode_str {
            "download" => 1195,
            "static_ip" => 1196,
            _ => 1194,
        };
        ("udp", server_host, p)
    };

    let static_ip_directives = if mode_str == "static_ip" {
        r#"
# ==============================================================================
# Strict DNS Leak & IP Isolation Directives (Trading & AI Safety)
# ==============================================================================
block-outside-dns
redirect-gateway def1
dhcp-option DNS 1.1.1.1
dhcp-option DNS 1.0.0.1
pull-filter ignore "ifconfig-ipv6"
pull-filter ignore "route-ipv6"
"#
    } else {
        ""
    };

    format!(
        r#"# ==============================================================================
# Sentra Platform - OpenVPN Client Profile
# Mode: {mode_str} [{mode_label}]
# Protocol: {protocol}
# Tier: {tier} | Token: {token}
# Daily Limits: Web Browsing (2048 MB) | Download (3072 MB)
# Generated: {now}
# ==============================================================================
client
dev tun
proto {proto_str}
remote {target_host} {target_port}
resolv-retry infinite
nobind
persist-key
persist-tun
remote-cert-tls server
cipher AES-256-GCM
auth SHA256
verb 3
auth-user-pass
{static_ip_directives}
# Embedded Public Key Infrastructure (PKI)
<ca>
-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----
MIIDSzCCAjOgAwIBAgIUW8d0Q3k9M1A2B3C4D5E6F7A8B9C0...[SENTRA-ROOT-CA]...
-----END CERTIFICATE-----
</ca>

<cert>
-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE-----
MIIDRjCCAi6gAwIBAgIUX9e1R4l0N2B3C4D5E6F7A8B9C0D...[SENTRA-CLIENT-CERT]...
-----END CERTIFICATE-----
</cert>

<key>
-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----
MIIEvgIBADANBgkqhkiG9w0BAQEFAASCBKgwggSkAgEAAoIBAQC...[SENTRA-CLIENT-KEY]...
-----END PRIVATE KEY-----
</key>

<tls-crypt>
-----BEGIN OpenVPN Static key V1-----
a1b2c3d4e5f60718293a4b5c6d7e8f90a1b2c3d4e5f60718293a4b5c6d7e8f90
-----END OpenVPN Static key V1-----
</tls-crypt>
"#,
        mode_str = mode_str,
        mode_label = mode_label,
        protocol = protocol,
        tier = subscription.tier,
        token = subscription.token,
        now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
        proto_str = proto_str,
        target_host = target_host,
        target_port = target_port,
        static_ip_directives = static_ip_directives
    )
}

/// Generates an Apple iOS/macOS .mobileconfig profile for IKEv2 one-click installation
pub fn generate_mobileconfig_profile(
    subscription: &Subscription,
    mode: &str,
    server_host: &str,
) -> String {
    let mode_str = if mode.is_empty() { "web_browsing" } else { mode };
    let mode_title = match mode_str {
        "download" => "Download",
        "static_ip" => "Static IP",
        "gaming" => "Gaming",
        _ => "Web Browsing",
    };

    let profile_uuid = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let vpn_uuid = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();

    let dns_block = if mode_str == "static_ip" {
        r#"                <key>DNS</key>
                <dict>
                    <key>ServerAddresses</key>
                    <array>
                        <string>1.1.1.1</string>
                        <string>1.0.0.1</string>
                    </array>
                    <key>SupplementalMatchDomains</key>
                    <array>
                        <string></string>
                    </array>
                </dict>"#
    } else {
        ""
    };

    format!(
        r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">

    <integer>1</integer>

            <key>PayloadUUID</key>
            <string>{vpn_uuid}</string>
            <key>PayloadDisplayName</key>
            <string>Sentra {mode_title} (IKEv2)</string>
            <key>UserDefinedName</key>
            <string>Sentra {mode_title}</string>
            <key>VPNType</key>
            <string>IKEv2</string>
            <key>IKEv2</key>
            <dict>
                <key>RemoteAddress</key>
                <string>{server_host}</string>
                <key>RemoteIdentifier</key>
                <string>{server_host}</string>
                <key>LocalIdentifier</key>
                <string>{token}</string>
                <key>AuthenticationMethod</key>
                <string>None</string>
                <key>ExtendedAuthEnabled</key>
                <integer>1</integer>
                <key>AuthName</key>
                <string>{token}</string>
                <key>AuthPassword</key>
                <string>{token}</string>
                <key>IKESecurityAssociationParameters</key>
                <dict>
                    <key>EncryptionAlgorithm</key>
                    <string>AES-256-GCM</string>
                    <key>IntegrityAlgorithm</key>
                    <string>SHA2-256</string>
                    <key>DiffieHellmanGroup</key>
                    <integer>14</integer>
                </dict>
                <key>ChildSecurityAssociationParameters</key>
                <dict>
                    <key>EncryptionAlgorithm</key>
                    <string>AES-256-GCM</string>
                    <key>IntegrityAlgorithm</key>
                    <string>SHA2-256</string>
                    <key>DiffieHellmanGroup</key>
                    <integer>14</integer>
                </dict>
                <key>DeadPeerDetectionRate</key>
                <string>Medium</string>
                <key>DisableMOBIKE</key>
                <integer>0</integer>
                <key>DisableRedirect</key>
                <integer>0</integer>
                <key>EnablePFS</key>
                <integer>1</integer>
                <key>UseConfigurationAttributeInternalIPSubnet</key>
                <integer>1</integer>
{dns_block}
            </dict>
            <key>IPv4</key>
            <dict>
                <key>OverridePrimary</key>
                <integer>1</integer>
            </dict>
        </dict>
    </array>

</plist>"#,
        mode_title = mode_title,
        mode_str = mode_str,

        dns_block = dns_block
    )

=== FILE: sentra-core\src\config.rs ===
use std::env;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Config {
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub base_url: String,
    pub database_url: String,
    pub teloxide_token: Option<String>,
    pub teloxide_proxy: Option<String>,
    pub admin_chat_id: i64,
    pub pro_subscription_price: i64,
}

impl Config {
    pub fn from_env() -> Self {
        dotenvy::dotenv().ok();

        let host = env::var("HOST").unwrap_or_else(|_| "127.0.0.1".to_string());
        let port = env::var("PORT")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|p| p.parse::<u16>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(8080);

        let base_url = env::var("BASE_URL")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("http://{}:{}", host, port));

        let database_url = env::var("DATABASE_URL")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "sqlite://sentra_local.db?mode=rwc".to_string());

        let teloxide_token = env::var("TELOXIDE_TOKEN")
            .ok()
            .filter(|t| !t.trim().is_empty() && !t.contains("SAMPLE"));

        let teloxide_proxy = env::var("TELOXIDE_PROXY")
            .ok()
            .map(|p| p.trim().to_string())
            .filter(|p| !p.is_empty());

        let admin_chat_id = env::var("ADMIN_CHAT_ID")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|id| id.parse::<i64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);

        let pro_subscription_price = env::var("PRO_SUBSCRIPTION_PRICE")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|p| p.parse::<i64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(150_000);

        Self {
            host,
            port,
            base_url,
            database_url,
            teloxide_token,
            teloxide_proxy,
            admin_chat_id,
            pro_subscription_price,
        }
    }
}

impl IntoResponse for CoreError {
    fn into_response(self) -> Response {
        let (status, error_code, message) = match &self {
            CoreError::Database(e) => {
                tracing::error!("Database query failed: {:?}", e);
                (
                    StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                    "DATABASE_ERROR",
                    "A database error occurred while processing your request.".to_string(),
                )
            }
            CoreError::Reqwest(e) => {
                tracing::error!("HTTP upstream request failed: {:?}", e);
                (
                    StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
                    "UPSTREAM_GATEWAY_ERROR",
                    "Failed to communicate with network upstream.".to_string(),
                )
            }
            CoreError::AuthFailed(msg) => (
                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,
                "AUTH_FAILED",
                msg.clone(),
            ),
            CoreError::UserNotFound => (
                StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
                "USER_NOT_FOUND",
                "The requested user account does not exist.".to_string(),
            ),
            CoreError::SubscriptionNotFound => (
                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,
                "SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_FOUND",
                "The provided subscription token is invalid or has been revoked.".to_string(),
            ),
            CoreError::SubscriptionExpired(date) => (
                StatusCode::PAYMENT_REQUIRED,
                "SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRED",
                format!("Your subscription expired on {}. Please upgrade to continue.", date),
            ),
            CoreError::QuotaExceeded { used, limit } => (
                StatusCode::PAYMENT_REQUIRED,
                "QUOTA_EXCEEDED",
                format!("Data limit exhausted: used {} of {} bytes.", used, limit),
            ),
            CoreError::DailyQuotaExceeded { mode, used_mb, limit_mb } => (
                StatusCode::PAYMENT_REQUIRED,
                "DAILY_QUOTA_EXCEEDED",

                "MAX_DEVICES_REACHED",

                    "Ø³Ù‚Ù Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² ØªØ¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù…ØªØµÙ„ (Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ú©Ø«Ø± {} Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡) ØªÚ©Ù…ÛŒÙ„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª. Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡ Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ØŒ ÛŒÚ©ÛŒ Ø§Ø² Ø¯Ø³ØªÚ¯Ø§Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ø§Ø² ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª Ø§Ù¾Ù„ÛŒÚ©ÛŒØ´Ù† ÛŒØ§ Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… Ø­Ø°Ù Ù†Ù…Ø§ÛŒÛŒØ¯.",
                    max
                ),
            ),
            CoreError::UserBanned(reason) => (
                StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
                "USER_BANNED",
                format!("Account suspended: {}", reason),
            ),
            CoreError::DeviceLimitExceeded(max) => (
                StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
                "DEVICE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
                format!("Concurrent device limit reached (max: {} devices).", max),
            ),
            CoreError::Internal(msg) => {
                tracing::error!("Internal error: {}", msg);
                (
                    StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                    "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                    "An unexpected error occurred.".to_string(),
                )
            }
            CoreError::BadRequest(msg) => (
                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
                "BAD_REQUEST",
                msg.clone(),
            ),
            CoreError::TooManyRequests(msg) => (
                StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
                "TOO_MANY_REQUESTS",
                msg.clone(),
            ),
            CoreError::ProxyError(msg) => (
                StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                "PROXY_ERROR",
                format!("Telegram proxy error: {}", msg),
            ),
        };

        let body = Json(json!({
            "success": false,
            "error": error_code,
            "message": message,
        }));

        (status, body).into_response()
    }
}

use config::Config;

use tracing::info;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    // 1. Initialize structured tracing logger
    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_env_filter(
            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| "info,sentra_core=debug".into()),
        )
        .init();

    info!("===============================================================");
    info!("   Starting Sentra Platform Core Backend & Telegram Bot Daemon ");
    info!("===============================================================");

    // 2. Load environment settings (.env with dotenvy)
    let config = Config::from_env();
    info!("Loaded configuration. Listening on {}:{}", config.host, config.port);

    // 3. Connect to SQLite and execute auto-migrations + default seed
    let pool = db::init_pool(&config.database_url).await?;
    db::run_migrations_and_seed(&pool).await?;

    // Synchronize sing-box server bridge configuration on startup
    if let Err(e) = bridge_sync::sync_server_bridge_config(&pool).await {
        tracing::warn!("Startup bridge sync warning: {:?}", e);
    }

    // 4. Spawn Telegram Bot in parallel Long-Polling mode (with proxy support)
    let bot_pool = pool.clone();
    let bot_token = config.teloxide_token.clone();
    let bot_proxy = config.teloxide_proxy.clone();
    let admin_chat_id = config.admin_chat_id;
    let base_url = config.base_url.clone();
    let pro_price = config.pro_subscription_price;

    tokio::spawn(async move {
        bot::run_telegram_bot(bot_token, bot_proxy, bot_pool, admin_chat_id, base_url, pro_price).await;
    });

    // 5. Initialize Anomaly Detector and build Axum REST API router
    let anomaly_detector = anomaly_detector::AnomalyDetector::new();
    let bot_for_api = match &config.teloxide_token {
        Some(t) if !t.trim().is_empty() && !t.contains("SAMPLE") => Some(teloxide::Bot::new(t)),
        _ => None,
    };

    // 6. Spawn 12-hour SQLite Hot Backup Task to Admin Telegram
    let backup_pool = pool.clone();
    let backup_bot = bot_for_api.clone();
    let backup_admin_chat_id = config.admin_chat_id;
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut interval = tokio::time::interval(std::time::Duration::from_secs(12 * 3600));
        // First tick completes immediately, wait for the first 12-hour period
        interval.tick().await;

        loop {
            interval.tick().await;
            tracing::info!("Starting scheduled 12-hour SQLite database hot backup...");
            let backup_filename = "sentra_backup.db";
            if std::path::Path::new(backup_filename).exists() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(backup_filename).await;
            }

            match sqlx::query("VACUUM INTO 'sentra_backup.db'").execute(&backup_pool).await {
                Ok(_) => {
                    tracing::info!("SQLite VACUUM INTO completed successfully.");
                    match tokio::fs::read(backup_filename).await {
                        Ok(data) => {
                            let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(backup_filename).await;
                            if let Some(bot) = &backup_bot {
                                if backup_admin_chat_id != 0 {
                                    use teloxide::prelude::*;
                                    use teloxide::types::{ChatId, InputFile, ParseMode};
                                    let now_tehran = chrono::Utc::now().with_timezone(&chrono_tz::Asia::Tehran);
                                    let file_size_kb = data.len() / 1024;
                                    let filename = format!("sentra_backup_{}.db", now_tehran.format("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"));
                                    let input_file = InputFile::memory(data).file_name(filename);
                                    let caption = format!(
                                        "ðŸ“¦ <b>Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†â€ŒÚ¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø¯ÛŒØªØ§Ø¨ÛŒØ³ Sentra</b>\n\nâ° ØªØ§Ø±ÛŒØ® Ùˆ Ø³Ø§Ø¹Øª (Ø§ÛŒØ±Ø§Ù†): <code>{}</code>\nðŸ“Š Ø­Ø¬Ù… ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„: <code>{} KB</code>\nðŸ”’ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª: Ø³Ø§Ù„Ù… Ùˆ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù‚ÙÙ„ (VACUUM)",
                                        now_tehran.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                                        file_size_kb
                                    );
                                    if let Err(e) = bot
                                        .send_document(ChatId(backup_admin_chat_id), input_file)
                                        .caption(caption)
                                        .parse_mode(ParseMode::Html)
                                        .await
                                    {
                                        tracing::error!("Failed to send database backup to Telegram admin: {:?}", e);
                                    } else {
                                        tracing::info!("Sentra database backup successfully sent to Telegram admin.");
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::error!("Failed to read backup database file: {:?}", e);
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::error!("Failed to execute SQLite VACUUM INTO: {:?}", e);
                }
            }
        }
    });

    // 5c. Spawn periodic Quality, Security & Auto-Ranking Engine (every 15 minutes)
    // Runs jitter benchmark, Gemini/Trading readiness test, 48-hour dead nodes cleanup, clawback, and bridge auto-sync
    quality_engine::QualityEngine::start_quality_engine_worker(
        pool.clone(),
        bot_for_api.clone(),
        config.admin_chat_id,
    );

    let app = api::create_router(pool, anomaly_detector, bot_for_api, config.admin_chat_id, config.host.clone());

    // 6. Bind TCP listener on configured address (port 8080)
    let addr: SocketAddr = format!("{}:{}", config.host, config.port).parse()?;
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(addr).await?;
    info!("ðŸš€ Sentra Core REST API running on http://{}", addr);
    info!("ðŸ‘‰ Auth Code Login:     POST http://{}/api/v1/auth/code-login", addr);
    info!("ðŸ‘‰ Client Dynamic Config: POST http://{}/api/v1/client/config", addr);
    info!("ðŸ‘‰ V2Ray/Sing-box Sub:    GET  http://{}/api/v1/sub/{{token}}", addr);
    info!("ðŸ‘‰ System Health:         GET  http://{}/health", addr);

    axum::serve(listener, app)
        .with_graceful_shutdown(shutdown_signal())
        .await?;

    info!("Sentra Core server shutdown gracefully.");
    Ok(())
}

async fn shutdown_signal() {
    tokio::signal::ctrl_c()
        .await
        .expect("Failed to install Ctrl+C signal handler");
    info!("Termination signal received. Shutting down Sentra Core cleanly...");
}

use crate::db::{DbPool, Repository};
use base64::prelude::*;
use rand::Rng;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::net::{IpAddr, SocketAddr};

use teloxide::types::ParseMode;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

use tracing::{error, info};
use url::Url;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ParsedProxy {
    pub tag: String,
    pub protocol: String,
    pub server_address: String,
    pub server_port: u16,
    pub config_json: serde_json::Value,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SubscriptionImportResult {
    pub source_id: i64,
    pub total_nodes: usize,
    pub healthy_nodes: usize,
    pub invalid_nodes: usize,
    pub country_counts: std::collections::HashMap<String, usize>,
}

pub struct OutboundAnalyzer;

impl OutboundAnalyzer {
    /// Parse raw text (link or singbox json) into a standardized proxy definition
    pub fn parse_input(raw: &str) -> Result<ParsedProxy, String> {
        let trimmed = raw.trim();

        // 1. Check if input is Sing-box JSON format
        if trimmed.starts_with('{') && trimmed.ends_with('}') {
            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(trimmed) {
                let proto = val.get("type")
                    .or_else(|| val.get("protocol"))
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or("unknown")
                    .to_string();
                let server = val.get("server")
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .ok_or("ÙÛŒÙ„Ø¯ server Ø¯Ø± JSON ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯")?
                    .to_string();
                let port = val.get("server_port")
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
                    .unwrap_or(443) as u16;
                let tag = val.get("tag")
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or("donated-singbox")
                    .to_string();

                return Ok(ParsedProxy {
                    tag,
                    protocol: proto,
                    server_address: server,
                    server_port: port,
                    config_json: val,
                });
            }
        }

        // 2. Parse URI formats (vless://, trojan://, hy2://, hysteria2://, tuic://, ss://, vmess://)
        if trimmed.starts_with("vmess://") {
            return Self::parse_vmess(trimmed);
        }

        if trimmed.starts_with("ss://") {
            return Self::parse_shadowsocks(trimmed);
        }

        if trimmed.starts_with("vless://")
            || trimmed.starts_with("trojan://")
            || trimmed.starts_with("hy2://")
            || trimmed.starts_with("hysteria2://")
            || trimmed.starts_with("tuic://")
        {
            return Self::parse_standard_uri(trimmed);
        }

        Err("ÙØ±Ù…Øª Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú© Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÚ©Ø³ÛŒ Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ù†Ø´Ø¯. Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú©â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡: vless://, vmess://, trojan://, hy2://, ss:// ÛŒØ§ Sing-box JSON".to_string())
    }

    /// Parse standard URI schemes: vless, trojan, hy2/hysteria2, tuic
    fn parse_standard_uri(raw: &str) -> Result<ParsedProxy, String> {
        let url = Url::parse(raw).map_err(|e| format!("Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú© Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÚ©Ø³ÛŒ Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª: {}", e))?;
        let scheme = url.scheme().to_lowercase();
        let protocol = match scheme.as_str() {
            "hy2" | "hysteria2" => "hysteria2".to_string(),
            other => other.to_string(),
        };

        let server_address = url.host_str().ok_or("Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø¯Ø± Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú© Ù…ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª")?.to_string();
        let server_port = url.port().unwrap_or(443);
        let tag = url.fragment().unwrap_or("donated-node").to_string();
        let tag = urlencoding_decode(&tag);

        let mut config = serde_json::json!({
            "type": protocol,
            "tag": tag,
            "server": server_address,
            "server_port": server_port,
        });

        // Add credentials based on protocol
        if protocol == "vless" {
            let uuid = url.username();
            config["uuid"] = serde_json::Value::String(uuid.to_string());
        } else if protocol == "trojan" || protocol == "hysteria2" {
            let password = if !url.password().unwrap_or("").is_empty() {
                url.password().unwrap_or("")
            } else {
                url.username()
            };
            config["password"] = serde_json::Value::String(password.to_string());
        }

        // Parse query params (sni, security, type, etc.)
        let query_pairs: Vec<(String, String)> = url.query_pairs().into_owned().collect();
        let mut tls_obj = serde_json::json!({ "enabled": true });
        let mut transport_obj = serde_json::json!({});

        for (k, v) in query_pairs {
            match k.as_str() {
                "sni" | "peer" => {
                    tls_obj["server_name"] = serde_json::Value::String(v);
                }
                "security" => {
                    if v == "reality" {
                        tls_obj["reality"] = serde_json::json!({ "enabled": true });
                    }
                }
                "pbk" => {
                    if tls_obj.get("reality").is_none() {
                        tls_obj["reality"] = serde_json::json!({ "enabled": true });
                    }
                    tls_obj["reality"]["public_key"] = serde_json::Value::String(v);
                }
                "sid" => {
                    if tls_obj.get("reality").is_none() {
                        tls_obj["reality"] = serde_json::json!({ "enabled": true });
                    }
                    tls_obj["reality"]["short_id"] = serde_json::Value::String(v);
                }
                "type" => {
                    transport_obj["type"] = serde_json::Value::String(v);
                }
                "path" => {
                    transport_obj["path"] = serde_json::Value::String(v);
                }
                "host" => {
                    transport_obj["headers"] = serde_json::json!({ "Host": v });
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        if tls_obj.as_object().map(|o| o.len() > 1).unwrap_or(false) {
            config["tls"] = tls_obj;
        }
        if transport_obj.as_object().map(|o| !o.is_empty()).unwrap_or(false) {
            config["transport"] = transport_obj;
        }

        Ok(ParsedProxy {
            tag,
            protocol,
            server_address,
            server_port,
            config_json: config,
        })
    }

    /// Parse VMess URI
    fn parse_vmess(raw: &str) -> Result<ParsedProxy, String> {
        let b64_part = raw.trim_start_matches("vmess://");
        let decoded = decode_b64(b64_part)?;
        let json_str = String::from_utf8(decoded).map_err(|_| "Ù…Ø­ØªÙˆØ§ÛŒ vmess ÙØ±Ù…Øª Ù…ØªÙ†ÛŒ Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯")?;

            .map_err(|e| format!("Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø¯ÛŒÚ©ÙˆØ¯ Ú©Ø±Ø¯Ù† JSON Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯ vmess: {}", e))?;

            .and_then(|s| s.as_str())
            .ok_or("ÙÛŒÙ„Ø¯ add (Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ±) Ø¯Ø± vmess ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯")?
            .to_string();

            p as u16
        } else if let Some(p_str) = v.get("port").and_then(|p| p.as_str()) {
            p_str.parse::<u16>().unwrap_or(443)
        } else {
            443
        };

        let uuid = v.get("id").and_then(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let tag = v.get("ps").and_then(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("donated-vmess").to_string();

        let config = serde_json::json!({
            "type": "vmess",
            "tag": tag,
            "server": server_address,
            "server_port": server_port,
            "uuid": uuid,
            "alter_id": v.get("aid").and_then(|a| a.as_u64()).unwrap_or(0),
        });

        Ok(ParsedProxy {
            tag,
            protocol: "vmess".to_string(),
            server_address,
            server_port,
            config_json: config,
        })
    }

    /// Parse Shadowsocks URI (SIP002 standard: ss://base64@host:port#tag)
    fn parse_shadowsocks(raw: &str) -> Result<ParsedProxy, String> {
        let without_prefix = raw.trim_start_matches("ss://");
        let (main_part, tag) = if let Some((m, t)) = without_prefix.split_once('#') {
            (m, urlencoding_decode(t))
        } else {
            (without_prefix, "donated-ss".to_string())
        };

        let (method, password, server, port) = if let Some((userinfo, hostport)) = main_part.split_once('@') {
            let decoded_userinfo = decode_b64(userinfo).unwrap_or_else(|_| userinfo.as_bytes().to_vec());
            let userinfo_str = String::from_utf8(decoded_userinfo).unwrap_or_default();
            let (m, p) = userinfo_str.split_once(':').unwrap_or(("aes-256-gcm", "password"));
            let (h, pt) = hostport.split_once(':').unwrap_or((hostport, "443"));
            (m.to_string(), p.to_string(), h.to_string(), pt.parse::<u16>().unwrap_or(443))
        } else {
            // Entire string may be base64
            let decoded = decode_b64(main_part)?;
            let decoded_str = String::from_utf8(decoded).map_err(|_| "Ù…Ø­ØªÙˆØ§ÛŒ Shadowsocks Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª")?;
            let (m, rest) = decoded_str.split_once(':').ok_or("ÙØ±Ù…Øª Shadowsocks Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª")?;
            let (p, hostport) = rest.split_once('@').ok_or("ÙØ±Ù…Øª Shadowsocks Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª")?;
            let (h, pt) = hostport.split_once(':').unwrap_or((hostport, "443"));
            (m.to_string(), p.to_string(), h.to_string(), pt.parse::<u16>().unwrap_or(443))
        };

        let config = serde_json::json!({
            "type": "shadowsocks",
            "tag": tag,
            "server": server,
            "server_port": port,
            "method": method,
            "password": password
        });

        Ok(ParsedProxy {
            tag,
            protocol: "shadowsocks".to_string(),
            server_address: server,
            server_port: port,
            config_json: config,
        })
    }

    /// Extract declared or detected capacity in GB from input text
    pub fn extract_capacity_gb(raw_text: &str) -> i64 {
        let lower = raw_text.to_lowercase();
        // Check tokens like "250gb", "300g", "500"
        for part in lower.split_whitespace() {
            let clean = part.trim_end_matches("gb").trim_end_matches('g');
            if let Ok(num) = clean.parse::<i64>() {
                if (10..=100_000).contains(&num) {
                    return num;
                }
            }
        }
        // Check tag or URL query params (e.g. tag having 250GB)
        for segment in lower.split(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric()) {
            if let Ok(num) = segment.parse::<i64>() {
                if (200..=10_000).contains(&num) {
                    return num;
                }
            }
        }
        0
    }

    /// Anti-SSRF check: Ensures destination IP is not a private, loopback, or reserved range
    pub async fn check_anti_ssrf(host: &str) -> Result<IpAddr, String> {
        let addrs: Vec<SocketAddr> = timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(4),
            tokio::net::lookup_host(format!("{}:80", host))
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|_| "Ø®Ø·Ø§: ØªØ§ÛŒÙ…â€ŒØ§ÙˆØª Ø¯Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± (DNS Timeout)".to_string())?
        .map_err(|e| format!("Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÚ©Ø³ÛŒ Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„ ØªØ­Ù„ÛŒÙ„ (Resolve) Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª: {}", e))?
        .collect();

        if addrs.is_empty() {
            return Err("Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ IP Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯ Ù†Ø¸Ø± ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.".to_string());
        }

        let ip = addrs[0].ip();
        if is_private_or_restricted(&ip) {
            return Err(format!(
                "Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØª SSRF: Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± ({}) Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯Ù‡ Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ù…Ø­Ù„ÛŒ ØªØ¹Ù„Ù‚ Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯ Ùˆ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯.",
                ip
            ));
        }

        Ok(ip)
    }

    /// Run real benchmark: latency (TCP handshake) and speed throughput estimation
    pub async fn benchmark_proxy(ip: IpAddr, port: u16) -> (i64, f64, bool, Option<String>) {
        let socket_addr = SocketAddr::new(ip, port);
        let start = Instant::now();

        // 1. Measure TCP handshake latency
        let connect_res = timeout(Duration::from_millis(3000), TcpStream::connect(socket_addr)).await;
        let latency_ms = match connect_res {
            Ok(Ok(_stream)) => start.elapsed().as_millis() as i64,
            Ok(Err(e)) => {
                return (9999, 0.0, false, Some(format!("Ù¾ÙˆØ±Øª Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø¨Ø§Ø² Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª ÛŒØ§ ÙÛŒÙ„ØªØ± Ø§Ø³Øª: {}", e)));
            }
            Err(_) => {
                return (9999, 0.0, false, Some("ØªØ§ÛŒÙ…â€ŒØ§ÙˆØª Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± (Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Û³Û°Û°Û° Ù…ÛŒÙ„ÛŒâ€ŒØ«Ø§Ù†ÛŒÙ‡)".to_string()));
            }
        };

        if latency_ms > 2500 {
            return (latency_ms, 0.5, false, Some("Ù¾ÛŒÙ†Ú¯ Ø¨Ø³ÛŒØ§Ø± Ø¨Ø§Ù„Ø§ (>Û²ÛµÛ°Û°ms) - Ú©ÛŒÙÛŒØª Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø·Ù„ÙˆØ¨".to_string()));
        }

        // 2. Measure / estimate speed based on network round-trip & latency jitter
        // A low latency handshake (<150ms) corresponds to ~18-30 Mbps international throughput.
        // Latency between 150-400ms corresponds to ~8-18 Mbps.
        // Latency between 400-1000ms corresponds to ~3-8 Mbps.
        // Above 1000ms corresponds to ~1-3 Mbps.
        let speed_mbps = if latency_ms < 150 {
            24.5 - (latency_ms as f64 * 0.05)
        } else if latency_ms < 400 {
            14.0 - ((latency_ms - 150) as f64 * 0.02)
        } else if latency_ms < 800 {
            7.5 - ((latency_ms - 400) as f64 * 0.008)
        } else {
            2.5 - ((latency_ms - 800) as f64 * 0.001).min(1.5)
        };
        let speed_mbps = (speed_mbps * 10.0).round() / 10.0;

        (latency_ms, speed_mbps.max(0.5), true, None)
    }

    /// Anti-Hijack & Security Policy: Rejects unencrypted protocols or plain insecure configs
    pub fn validate_security_and_encryption(parsed: &ParsedProxy) -> Result<(), String> {
        match parsed.protocol.to_lowercase().as_str() {
            "http" | "socks" => {
                let has_tls = parsed.config_json.get("tls")
                    .and_then(|t| t.get("enabled"))
                    .and_then(|e| e.as_bool())
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                if !has_tls {
                    return Err("Ù¾Ø±ÙˆØªÚ©Ù„â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù…ØªÙ†ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ ØºÛŒØ±Ø§Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯ (HTTP/Socks Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† TLS) Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ (Anti-Hijack) Ù¾Ø°ÛŒØ±ÙØªÙ‡ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´ÙˆÙ†Ø¯.".to_string());
                }
            }
            "vless" => {
                let tls_enabled = parsed.config_json.get("tls")
                    .and_then(|t| t.get("enabled"))
                    .and_then(|e| e.as_bool())
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                let reality_enabled = parsed.config_json.pointer("/tls/reality/enabled")
                    .and_then(|r| r.as_bool())
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                if !tls_enabled && !reality_enabled {
                    return Err("Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ VLESS Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯Ù† Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ TLS ÛŒØ§ Reality Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„ÛŒÙ„ Ø¹Ø¯Ù… Ø±Ù…Ø²Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù†Ø¯.".to_string());
                }
            }
            "vmess" => {
                let security = parsed.config_json.get("security").and_then(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("auto");
                if security == "none" {
                    return Err("Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ VMess Ø¨Ø§ Ø±Ù…Ø²Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ (security: none) Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Ù†ÛŒØ³ØªÙ†Ø¯.".to_string());
                }
            }
            "shadowsocks" => {
                let method = parsed.config_json.get("method").and_then(|m| m.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                if method.is_empty() || method == "none" || method == "plain" {
                    return Err("Ø±ÙˆØ´ Ø±Ù…Ø²Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Shadowsocks Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± ÛŒØ§ Ø®Ø§Ù…ÙˆØ´ Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string());
                }
            }
            "trojan" | "hysteria2" | "tuic" => {
                // Standard protocols with mandatory TLS or QUIC encryption
            }
            _ => {
                return Err("Ù¾Ø±ÙˆØªÚ©Ù„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡ Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ Ø´Ø¨Ú©Ù‡ Sentra Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª.".to_string());
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Isolated benchmark runner: spawns sing-box on a random high port (30000..=60000)
    /// with strict 12-second timeout and stripped environment, falling back safely if sing-box binary is absent.
    pub async fn benchmark_proxy_isolated(
        ip: IpAddr,
        port: u16,
        parsed_config: &serde_json::Value,
    ) -> (i64, f64, bool, Option<String>) {
        let random_port: u16 = rand::thread_rng().gen_range(30000..=60000);
        let temp_dir = std::env::temp_dir();
        let config_file_name = format!("sentra_bench_{}_{}.json", std::process::id(), random_port);
        let config_path = temp_dir.join(config_file_name);

        let minimal_config = serde_json::json!({
            "log": { "level": "panic" },
            "inbounds": [
                {
                    "type": "socks",
                    "tag": "bench-in",
                    "listen": "127.0.0.1",
                    "listen_port": random_port
                }
            ],
            "outbounds": [
                parsed_config
            ]
        });

        if let Ok(content) = serde_json::to_string(&minimal_config) {
            let _ = tokio::fs::write(&config_path, content).await;
        }

        let start = Instant::now();
        // Attempt to run sing-box in isolated child process with 12s timeout and cleared environment
        let child_res = tokio::process::Command::new("sing-box")
            .arg("run")
            .arg("-c")
            .arg(&config_path)
            .env_clear()
            .kill_on_drop(true)
            .spawn();

        let benchmark_result = match child_res {
            Ok(mut child) => {
                let bench_task = async {
                    // Give child process 200ms to spin up listening port
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
                    let local_addr = SocketAddr::new(IpAddr::V4(std::net::Ipv4Addr::new(127, 0, 0, 1)), random_port);
                    let connect_res = timeout(Duration::from_millis(3000), TcpStream::connect(local_addr)).await;
                    let latency = match connect_res {
                        Ok(Ok(_)) => start.elapsed().as_millis() as i64,
                        _ => 9999,
                    };
                    let _ = child.kill().await;
                    latency
                };

                match timeout(Duration::from_secs(12), bench_task).await {
                    Ok(latency) if latency < 2500 => {
                        let speed = if latency < 150 {
                            24.5 - (latency as f64 * 0.05)
                        } else if latency < 400 {
                            14.0 - ((latency - 150) as f64 * 0.02)
                        } else if latency < 800 {
                            7.5 - ((latency - 400) as f64 * 0.008)
                        } else {
                            2.5 - ((latency - 800) as f64 * 0.001).min(1.5)
                        };
                        let speed = (speed * 10.0).round() / 10.0;
                        (latency, speed.max(0.5), true, None)
                    }
                    Ok(_) => (9999, 0.0, false, Some("ØªØ§ÛŒÙ…â€ŒØ§ÙˆØª Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø§ÛŒÙ†Ø¨Ø§Ù†Ø¯ ØªØ³ØªÛŒ sing-box".to_string())),
                    Err(_) => (9999, 0.0, false, Some("ØªØ§ÛŒÙ…â€ŒØ§ÙˆØª Ø¨Ù†Ú†Ù…Ø§Ø±Ú© Ø§ÛŒØ²ÙˆÙ„Ù‡ (Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Û±Û² Ø«Ø§Ù†ÛŒÙ‡)".to_string())),
                }
            }
            Err(_) => {
                // If sing-box binary is not in PATH (e.g. host without sing-box binary), fall back safely to TCP handshake
                Self::benchmark_proxy(ip, port).await
            }
        };

        let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&config_path).await;
        benchmark_result
    }

    /// Full asynchronous pipeline: Parse -> Anti-SSRF -> Deduplicate -> Benchmark -> Save & Reward
    pub async fn process_donation(
        pool: DbPool,
        bot: Bot,
        donor_user_id: Option<i64>,
        donor_telegram_id: Option<i64>,
        raw_text: String,
    ) {
        info!("Beginning processing donated outbound for donor user {:?}", donor_user_id);

        // 1. Parse link or JSON
        let parsed = match Self::parse_input(&raw_text) {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(e) => {
                if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                    let text = format!("âŒ <b>Ø§Ù‡Ø¯Ø§ÛŒ Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚:</b>\n\n{}", e);
                    let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
                }
                return;
            }
        };

        if let Err(e) = Self::validate_security_and_encryption(&parsed) {
            if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                let text = format!("â›” <b>Ø±Ø¯ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± (Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ Anti-Hijack):</b>\n\n{}", e);
                let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
            }
            return;
        }

        // 3. Anti-SSRF check & IP resolution
        let ip = match Self::check_anti_ssrf(&parsed.server_address).await {
            Ok(ip) => ip,
            Err(e) => {
                if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                    let text = format!("â›” <b>Ø±Ø¯ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± (Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØªÛŒ DNS / SSRF):</b>\n\n{}", e);
                    let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
                }
                return;
            }
        };

        // 4. Deep Outbound Fingerprinting (SHA-256 of resolved_ip + port + protocol + secret)
        let secret = parsed.config_json.get("uuid")
            .or_else(|| parsed.config_json.get("password"))
            .or_else(|| parsed.config_json.pointer("/tls/reality/public_key"))
            .and_then(|s| s.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("");

        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        hasher.update(format!("{}:{}:{}:{}", ip, parsed.server_port, parsed.protocol.to_lowercase(), secret).as_bytes());
        let fingerprint = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

        // Deduplication check via Deep Fingerprint
        match Repository::is_fingerprint_duplicate(&pool, &fingerprint).await {
            Ok(true) => {
                if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                    let text = "âš ï¸ <b>Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± ØªÚ©Ø±Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø§Ø³Øª (Deep Fingerprint Match):</b>\n\nØ§ÛŒÙ† Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø¨Ø§ Ù…Ø´Ø®ØµØ§Øª ÙÙ†ÛŒ Ùˆ Ú©Ù„ÛŒØ¯ ÛŒÚ©Ø³Ø§Ù† Ù‚Ø¨Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø¯Ø± Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª (ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ù†Ø§Ù…ØŒ Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ù¾ÙˆØ´Ø´ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù…ØªØ±Ù‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¸Ø§Ù‡Ø±ÛŒ Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª).";
                    let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
                }
                return;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Database error checking fingerprint duplicate: {}", e);
            }
            Ok(false) => {}
        }

        // Also check server address and port uniqueness
        match Repository::is_outbound_duplicate(&pool, &parsed.server_address, parsed.server_port as i64).await {
            Ok(true) => {
                if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                    let text = "âš ï¸ <b>Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± ØªÚ©Ø±Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø§Ø³Øª:</b>\n\nØ§ÛŒÙ† Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ Ùˆ Ù¾ÙˆØ±Øª Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ù‚Ø¨Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø¯Ø± Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª Ùˆ Ø§Ù…Ú©Ø§Ù† Ø«Ø¨Øª Ù…Ø¬Ø¯Ø¯ ÙˆØ¬ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯.";
                    let _ = bot.send_message(ChatId(tid), text).parse_mode(ParseMode::Html).await;
                }
                return;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                error!("Database error checking duplicate: {}", e);
            }
            Ok(false) => {}
        }

            Self::benchmark_proxy_isolated(ip, parsed.server_port, &parsed.config_json).await;

            ("rejected".to_string(), "none".to_string())
        } else if speed_mbps >= 8.0 && latency_ms <= 800 {
            ("active".to_string(), "download".to_string())
        } else {
            ("active".to_string(), "web_browsing".to_string())
        };

        let declared_gb = Self::extract_capacity_gb(&raw_text);

        let is_reward_banned = if let Some(uid) = donor_user_id {
            Repository::is_user_reward_banned(&pool, uid).await.unwrap_or(false)
        } else {
            false
        };

        let (country_code, country_name) = Self::detect_country(ip, &parsed.tag).await;

        // 6. Save to database with fingerprint
        let save_res = Repository::save_donated_outbound(
            &pool,
            donor_user_id,
            &parsed.tag,
            &parsed.protocol,
            &parsed.server_address,
            parsed.server_port as i64,
            &config_str,
            latency_ms,
            speed_mbps,
            &assigned_tier,
            &status,
            Some(&raw_text),
            reject_reason.as_deref(),
            declared_gb,
            Some(&fingerprint),
            &country_code,
            &country_name,
        ).await;

        match save_res {
            Ok(outbound) => {
                info!("Saved donated outbound #{} with status {} and tier {}", outbound.id, status, assigned_tier);

                if status == "active" {
                    if let Some(tid) = donor_telegram_id {
                        let tier_fa = match assigned_tier.as_str() {
                            "download" => "ðŸš€ Ø­Ø§Ù„Øª Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ù„ÙˆØ¯ Ùˆ Ø§Ø³ØªØ±ÛŒÙ… Ù¾Ø±Ø³Ø±Ø¹Øª",

                        };

                        } else if declared_gb > 200 {

    /// Automatically detects country code and Persian name with flag based on tag and online IP Geo lookup
    pub async fn detect_country(ip: IpAddr, tag: &str) -> (String, String) {
        let tag_lower = tag.to_lowercase();

        // 1. Check tag heuristics (flags, country names, abbreviations)
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡©ðŸ‡ª") || tag_lower.contains("germany") || tag_lower.contains("de-") || tag_lower.contains("-de") || tag_lower.contains("frankfurt") || tag_lower.contains("Ø¢Ù„Ù…Ø§Ù†") {
            return ("DE".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("DE"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡³ðŸ‡±") || tag_lower.contains("netherlands") || tag_lower.contains("holland") || tag_lower.contains("nl-") || tag_lower.contains("-nl") || tag_lower.contains("amsterdam") || tag_lower.contains("Ù‡Ù„Ù†Ø¯") {
            return ("NL".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("NL"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡®") || tag_lower.contains("finland") || tag_lower.contains("fi-") || tag_lower.contains("-fi") || tag_lower.contains("helsinki") || tag_lower.contains("ÙÙ†Ù„Ø§Ù†Ø¯") {
            return ("FI".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("FI"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡ºðŸ‡¸") || tag_lower.contains("usa") || tag_lower.contains("united states") || tag_lower.contains("us-") || tag_lower.contains("-us") || tag_lower.contains("america") || tag_lower.contains("Ø¢Ù…Ø±ÛŒÚ©Ø§") {
            return ("US".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("US"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡«ðŸ‡·") || tag_lower.contains("france") || tag_lower.contains("fr-") || tag_lower.contains("-fr") || tag_lower.contains("paris") || tag_lower.contains("ÙØ±Ø§Ù†Ø³Ù‡") {
            return ("FR".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("FR"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¬ðŸ‡§") || tag_lower.contains("uk") || tag_lower.contains("united kingdom") || tag_lower.contains("london") || tag_lower.contains("Ø§Ù†Ú¯Ù„ÛŒØ³") {
            return ("GB".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("GB"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¹ðŸ‡·") || tag_lower.contains("turkey") || tag_lower.contains("istanbul") || tag_lower.contains("tr-") || tag_lower.contains("-tr") || tag_lower.contains("ØªØ±Ú©ÛŒÙ‡") {
            return ("TR".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("TR"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¸ðŸ‡ª") || tag_lower.contains("sweden") || tag_lower.contains("Ø³ÙˆØ¦Ø¯") {
            return ("SE".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("SE"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¨ðŸ‡­") || tag_lower.contains("switzerland") || tag_lower.contains("Ø³ÙˆØ¦ÛŒØ³") {
            return ("CH".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("CH"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¸ðŸ‡¬") || tag_lower.contains("singapore") || tag_lower.contains("Ø³Ù†Ú¯Ø§Ù¾ÙˆØ±") {
            return ("SG".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("SG"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¯ðŸ‡µ") || tag_lower.contains("japan") || tag_lower.contains("tokyo") || tag_lower.contains("Ú˜Ø§Ù¾Ù†") {
            return ("JP".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("JP"));
        }
        if tag.contains("ðŸ‡¨ðŸ‡¦") || tag_lower.contains("canada") || tag_lower.contains("Ú©Ø§Ù†Ø§Ø¯Ø§") {
            return ("CA".to_string(), Self::country_code_to_name("CA"));
        }

        // 2. Perform online GeoIP lookup with a short timeout if IP is public
        if !is_private_or_restricted(&ip) {
            let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
                .timeout(Duration::from_millis(2500))
                .build();
            if let Ok(c) = client {
                let url = format!("http://ip-api.com/json/{}?fields=status,country,countryCode", ip);
                if let Ok(resp) = c.get(&url).send().await {
                    if let Ok(json_val) = resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await {
                        if json_val.get("status").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) == Some("success") {
                            if let Some(code) = json_val.get("countryCode").and_then(|c| c.as_str()) {
                                let code_upper = code.to_uppercase();
                                let name = Self::country_code_to_name(&code_upper);
                                return (code_upper, name);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Fallback default
        ("DE".to_string(), "Ø¢Ù„Ù…Ø§Ù† ðŸ‡©ðŸ‡ª".to_string())
    }

    /// Import official outbounds from a Subscription URL with Anti-SSRF, 2MB size limit,
    /// 10s timeout, max 50 nodes limit, automated ping test, and country persistence.
    pub async fn import_subscription_url(
        pool: &DbPool,
        category: &str,
        sub_url: &str,
    ) -> Result<SubscriptionImportResult, String> {
        let trimmed_url = sub_url.trim();

        // 1. Validate URL scheme strictly https
        let parsed_url = Url::parse(trimmed_url).map_err(|e| format!("Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª: {}", e))?;

            return Err("Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØª Anti-SSRF: ÙÙ‚Ø· Ù¾Ø±ÙˆØªÚ©Ù„ Ø§Ù…Ù† https:// Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string());
        }

        // 2. Anti-SSRF Protection: resolve domain and verify each IP is public
        let addrs: Vec<SocketAddr> = timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(5),
            tokio::net::lookup_host(format!("{}:443", host))
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|_| "ØªØ§ÛŒÙ…â€ŒØ§ÙˆØª Ø¯Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† (DNS Timeout)".to_string())?
        .map_err(|e| format!("Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„ Resolve Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª: {}", e))?
        .collect();

            return Err("Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ø¢Ø¯Ø±Ø³ IP Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.".to_string());
        }

            if is_private_or_restricted(&addr.ip()) {
                return Err(format!(
                    "Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØª SSRF: Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ø¨Ù‡ IP ØºÛŒØ±Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² ({}) Ø§Ø´Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ø¯ Ùˆ Ù…Ø³Ø¯ÙˆØ¯ Ø´Ø¯.",
                    addr.ip()
                ));
            }
        }

        let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .user_agent("SentraCore/1.0 (SubscriptionClient)")
            .build()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø§ÛŒØ¬Ø§Ø¯ Ú©Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ†Øª HTTP: {}", e))?;

        let resp = client.get(trimmed_url)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø§ØªØµØ§Ù„ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù†: {}", e))?;

            return Err(format!("Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ HTTP {} Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ù†Ø¯.", resp.status()));
        }

            return Err("Ø§Ù…Ù†ÛŒØª: Ø­Ø¬Ù… ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† Ø¨ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ø² Ø³Ù‚Ù Ù…Ø¬Ø§Ø² Û² Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØª Ø§Ø³Øª.".to_string());
        }

        // 4. Extract individual proxy definitions
        let mut node_strings: Vec<String> = Vec::new();

        // Try decoding as base64 first
        if let Ok(decoded_bytes) = decode_b64(raw_text.trim()) {
            let decoded_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&decoded_bytes).to_string();
            for line in decoded_str.lines() {
                let l = line.trim();
                if !l.is_empty() {
                    node_strings.push(l.to_string());
                }
            }
        } else if raw_text.trim().starts_with('[') || raw_text.trim().starts_with('{') {
            // Sing-box JSON array or JSON config
            if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(raw_text.trim()) {
                if let Some(arr) = val.as_array() {
                    for item in arr {
                        node_strings.push(item.to_string());
                    }
                } else if let Some(outbounds) = val.get("outbounds").and_then(|o| o.as_array()) {
                    for item in outbounds {
                        node_strings.push(item.to_string());
                    }
                } else {
                    node_strings.push(val.to_string());
                }
            }
        }

        // If not base64 or json array, split lines as plaintext
        if node_strings.is_empty() {
            for line in raw_text.lines() {
                let l = line.trim();
                if !l.is_empty() {
                    node_strings.push(l.to_string());
                }
            }
        }

        let total_nodes = node_strings.len();
        if total_nodes == 0 {
            return Err("Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ú©Ø§Ù†ÙÛŒÚ¯ Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÚ©Ø³ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ù…Ø­ØªÙˆØ§ÛŒ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù† ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.".to_string());
        }

        let mut invalid_nodes = 0;
        let mut country_counts = std::collections::HashMap::new();

        for raw_node in &limited_nodes {
            let parsed = match Self::parse_input(raw_node) {
                Ok(p) => p,
                Err(_) => {
                    invalid_nodes += 1;
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // Security & Anti-Hijack validation
            if Self::validate_security_and_encryption(&parsed).is_err() {
                invalid_nodes += 1;
                continue;
            }

            // Anti-SSRF check on node server address
            let ip = match Self::check_anti_ssrf(&parsed.server_address).await {
                Ok(ip) => ip,
                Err(_) => {
                    invalid_nodes += 1;
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // Latency & benchmark
            let (latency_ms, speed_mbps, is_online, _err) = Self::benchmark_proxy(ip, parsed.server_port).await;
            if !is_online || latency_ms > 2500 {
                invalid_nodes += 1;
                continue;
            }

            // Country detection
            let (country_code, country_name) = Self::detect_country(ip, &parsed.tag).await;

            // Generate fingerprint
            let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
            hasher.update(format!("{}:{}:{}", parsed.protocol, parsed.server_address, parsed.server_port).as_bytes());
            let fingerprint = format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize());

            let label = if !parsed.tag.is_empty() && parsed.tag != "donated-singbox" && parsed.tag != "donated-node" {
                parsed.tag.clone()
            } else {
                format!("{}-{}", country_code, parsed.protocol)
            };

            // Insert official outbound
            let insert_res = Repository::insert_official_outbound(
                pool,
                &label,
                &parsed.tag,
                &parsed.protocol,
                &parsed.server_address,
                parsed.server_port as i64,
                &parsed.config_json.to_string(),
                latency_ms,
                speed_mbps,
                category,
                Some(raw_node),
                &fingerprint,
                "active",
                100,
                &country_code,
                &country_name,
            )
            .await;

            if insert_res.is_ok() {
                healthy_nodes += 1;
                *country_counts.entry(country_name).or_insert(0) += 1;
            } else {
                invalid_nodes += 1;
            }
        }

        // Save subscription source for periodic re-sync
        let source_id = Repository::save_subscription_source(
            pool,
            category,
            trimmed_url,
            Some(&format!("Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ù„ÛŒÙ†Ú© {}", category)),
            total_nodes as i64,
            healthy_nodes as i64,
        )
        .await
        .map_err(|e| format!("Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ù…Ù†Ø¨Ø¹ Ø³Ø§Ø¨Ø³Ú©Ø±ÛŒÙ¾Ø´Ù†: {}", e))?;

        let _ = crate::bridge_sync::sync_server_bridge_config(pool).await;

        Ok(SubscriptionImportResult {
            source_id,
            total_nodes,
            healthy_nodes,
            invalid_nodes,
            country_counts,
        })
    }
}

/// Simple URL decode helper for node tag
fn urlencoding_decode(input: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(input.len());
    let mut chars = input.chars().peekable();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        if c == '%' {
            let h1 = chars.next().unwrap_or('0');
            let h2 = chars.next().unwrap_or('0');
            if let Ok(byte) = u8::from_str_radix(&format!("{}{}", h1, h2), 16) {
                result.push(byte as char);
            }
        } else if c == '+' {
            result.push(' ');
        } else {
            result.push(c);
        }
    }
    result
}

/// Helper to decode base64 strings with or without padding, standard or url-safe
fn decode_b64(input: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let clean = input.trim().replace('-', "+").replace('_', "/");
    let padded = match clean.len() % 4 {
        2 => format!("{}==", clean),
        3 => format!("{}=", clean),
        _ => clean,
    };
    BASE64_STANDARD.decode(padded.as_bytes()).map_err(|e| format!("Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯ÛŒÚ©ÙˆØ¯ Base64: {}", e))
}

/// Checks if an IP is in private, loopback, link-local, carrier-grade NAT, or reserved ranges
fn is_private_or_restricted(ip: &IpAddr) -> bool {
    match ip {
        IpAddr::V4(ipv4) => {
            let octets = ipv4.octets();
            // Loopback (127.0.0.0/8)
            octets[0] == 127
            // Unspecified (0.0.0.0)
            || octets[0] == 0
            // Private 10.0.0.0/8
            || octets[0] == 10
            // Carrier Grade NAT 100.64.0.0/10 (100.64.0.0 - 100.127.255.255)
            || (octets[0] == 100 && octets[1] >= 64 && octets[1] <= 127)
            // Private 172.16.0.0/12 (172.16.0.0 - 172.31.255.255)
            || (octets[0] == 172 && octets[1] >= 16 && octets[1] <= 31)
            // Link-local 169.254.0.0/16
            || (octets[0] == 169 && octets[1] == 254)
            // Private 192.168.0.0/16
            || (octets[0] == 192 && octets[1] == 168)
            // Broadcast (255.255.255.255)
            || octets == [255, 255, 255, 255]
        }
        IpAddr::V6(ipv6) => {
            ipv6.is_loopback()
            || ipv6.is_unspecified()
            // Unique local fc00::/7 (fc00 - fdff)
            || (ipv6.segments()[0] & 0xfe00) == 0xfc00
            // Link-local fe80::/10 (fe80 - febf)
            || (ipv6.segments()[0] & 0xffc0) == 0xfe80
        }
    }
}
